Ash Ember [Common Talent, Flamecharm, Weapon] - The more wither your opponent has, the more damage your burn deals.

- At 90 FLM 90 WPN you gain a 0.0175 burn damage increase per 1 point of wither.
- Ash Ember caps at 15 total burn damage.
- Every Emperor Flame proc will increase your burn damage by 0.4735, or 1.3125 if you have Scorching Decay.
- Prerequisites: 90 Flamecharm, 90 Weapon (90 Light // 90 Medium // 90 Heavy)

Scorching Decay [Common Talent, Flamecharm, Weapon] - The wither dealt from your Emperor's Flame is massively increased.

- Increases the Wither application from your Emperor Flame from 25 to 75.
- Prerequisites: Emperor Flame, 100 Flamecharm, 100 Weapon (100 Light // 100 Medium // 100 Heavy)

### Miscellaneous

Brassneck [Common Talent, Strength, Fortitude] - Knocking an enemy grants you 20% damage resistance for 15 seconds.

- This does work on non humanoid monsters, granting you the damage resistance buff on kill.
- Prerequisites: 35 Fortitude, 30 Strength
- This Talent was previously named "Berserker."

Careful Handling [Quest Talent] - Reduce your chances of dropping ingredients on death.

- Prerequisite: Complete [Suri](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Suri)'s quest

Dragon's Song [Common Talent] - Using a mantra after an uppercut grants ether.

- Grants +20 Ether on proc.
- 2 second cooldown.

Full Reset [Common Talent] - Knocking an enemy resets your [resonance](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Resonance) cooldowns. This effect has a 60 second cooldown.

- Prerequisites: [Power](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Power) 16, Obtain resonance

Gourmand [Common Talent] - Your hunger and thirst gain from eating is increased. (+5 Carry Load, +1 Posture)

- Increases hunger and thirst gain by 20%.
- Having this alongside the [Gourmet](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Gourmet) Boon increases the hunger and thirst gain from eating by 80%.
- Prerequisite: [Power](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Power) 5
- The [Chef's Toque](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Chef%27s_Toque) equipment has this Talent.

Heavy Haul [Common Talent, Strength] - Enemies who carry you move significantly slower. Take slightly less posture damage from large enemies. Takes one to know one.

- Prerequisite: 15 Strength
- The [Grand Authority Plate](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Grand_Authority_Plate) equipment has this Talent.

Martyr [Common Talent] - When you're knocked [Unconscious](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Unconscious), enemies gain less health and posture, and allies around you are granted [Martyrdom](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Martyrdom) for 20s.

- Martyrdom grants +20% damage and damage reduction for 20 seconds.
- Martyrdom cannot be given to the player who knocked you, even if they are your ally. Additionally, Martyr does not consider yourself an ally, meaning the buffs won't be applied to yourself.
- Enemies who knock you restore only half of the posture and health they would normally obtain (50% -> 25%).
- If the enemy have the Talent Replenishing Knockout, both Talents negate each other, resulting in a normal health pack.
- Prerequisite: [Power](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Power) 3

Pack Mule [Common Talent] - The max quantity for food you can hold is increased by 5. (+10 Carry Load)

- Stacks with the [Boon](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Packmule) of the same name, increasing the maximum amount of food items you can carry in one stack from 10 to 20.
- Prerequisite: 5 Fortitude

Pleeksty's Will [Quest Talent] - You gain significantly more ether from consuming elemental ingredients.

- Consuming [Gale Stones](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Gale_Stone), [Heartstars](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Heartstar), [Dying Embers](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dying_Embers), [Spark Glands](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Spark_Gland), and [Frigid Prisms](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Frigid_Prism) gives significantly more Ether.
- Consuming elemental ingredients has a 20 second cooldown.
- Is also a requirement to fight [Aska](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Aska) in Firfire. Without either this Talent, Pleeksty's Faith, or the [Flame Worshipper Armor](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Flame_Worshipper_Armor) you will wipe through his flames.
- Prerequisite: "Ploom's Embers" Quest in [Etris](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Etris) with 40 Flamecharm OR 50 Charisma OR by completing quest requirements.

Polite Awakening [Common Talent] - Recover 15% of your max health after getting up from being downed. (Only Procs when below 20% HP) (+1 Passive Agility)

- The [Khan Pauldrons](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Khan_Pauldrons) and [Woodland Pauldrons](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Woodland_Pauldrons) equipment have this Talent.

Ready or Not [Common Talent, Fortitude] - The first attack to hit you while out of combat has its damage cut in half.

- Prerequisite: 20 Fortitude
- The [Royal Pathfinder](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Royal_Pathfinder) equipment has this Talent.

Replenishing Knockout [Common Talent] - You gain more health and posture from downing enemies.

- Knocking enemies will replenish 1.5x more of your health and posture (50% -> 75%).
- If the target has the Talent Martyr, both Talents negate each other, resulting in a normal health pack.
- Prerequisite: [Power](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Power) 3

Treefelling Blow [Quest Talent] - You can collect lumber from the trees you fell with your fists. It's only natural.

- Allows you to gain [Wood](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Wood) when you destroy trees with [Strong Left](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Strong_Left).
- Prerequisites: Use [Strong Left](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Strong_Left) on a tree and then speak to [Korin](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Korin) or [Drefa](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Drefa) to obtain the Treefelling Blow quest. Once you have the quest, destroy 5 more trees with Strong Left and return to the respective quest giver.

## Other

- Fold [Obtained through Power] - Discard your current hand of Talents. On your next Power Level, you are able to pick another hand of cards. (10 Cards total, 2 choices)

## Weapon Talents

Not to be confused with Talents that have a weapon requirement, these Talents will automatically be given to you upon equipping specific [weapons](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Weapons), but only exist while these weapons are equipped; using any ability that changes your equipped weapon will temporarily remove these Talents from your character. These Talents are often very potent, as they are the unique traits of the weapons themselves, though not every weapon with a unique trait has a Talent card to display it. Weapon Talents are listed in alphabetical order:

Ancient Metalwork: [Dissonant Chimecaller](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dissonant_Chimecaller)- Your critical hit can channel the effects of your [Ironsing](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ironsing) Rods and disrupt the [resonances](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Resonance) of others, disabling them for 25 seconds.

- Landing a weapon critical puts your opponent's [Resonance](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Resonance) on a 25 second cooldown. This does not require Ironsing Rods to proc.

- Assassin's Strike: [Silver Dagger](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Silver_Dagger)- Your [Rapid Slashes](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rapid_Slashes) now deals twice the amount of damage.

Battle Frenzy: [Master Hawk's Handaxe](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Master_Hawk%27s_Handaxe)- The grip on your weapon tightens, reduce the posture of all mantras you block with this effect scaling with the number of combat tags you have on your character.

- This reduces the posture damage of incoming Mantras by 1 (flat) per hit, per combat tag you have.
- If you have more combat tags than the Mantra does posture damage, this can go into the negatives and your posture will be restored instead.
- Works in PvE, but you cannot have more than 1 PvE combat tag, so it won't be very effective.

Belief: [Worshipper Longsword](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Worshipper_Longsword)- Your [medium weapon mantras](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Attunement-less#Medium_Weapon_Mantras) now deal extra damage based on how much Willpower you have.

- All of your Medium Weapon Mantras gain a second damage instance that scales directly on your Willpower investment. This deals physical damage.
- This deals 0.15 damage per 1 Willpower investment. At maximum this will deal an additional 15 damage at 100 Willpower, or up to 15.45 if you have 103 Willpower.
- This effect has no cooldown, allowing your [Master's Flourish](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Master%27s_Flourish) and [Twincleave](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Twincleave) to benefit from this effect twice per cast.
- This secondary damage instance cannot receive damage modifiers.

Blitz: [Forgotten Gladius](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Forgotten_Gladius)& [Kindred Edict](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Kindred_Edict)- Increase how quick you release your [Prominence Draw](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Prominence_Draw).

- Decreases Prominence Draw's windup by 0.1s (from 0.55s to 0.45s).
- Adds a new sound effect to Prominence Draw.

Blood Drinker: [Bloodbane](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Bloodbane)- Recover health on hit. When landing a critical, deal 30 bleed damage over 5 seconds.

Restores 4% of the user's maximum health upon landing M1 attacks.

- This also procs on [Silentheart](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Silentheart) abilities.

Bulwark Breaker: [Steel Maul](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Steel_Maul)& [Saint Jay's Hammer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Saint_Jay%27s_Hammer)- Increase the size of your [Pressure Blast](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Pressure_Blast), with the last hit of it dealing increased posture damage.

The final hit of your Pressure Blast gains 3x posture damage, going from 12 to 36 posture damage.

- Assuming all hits hit your opponent's block, your Pressure Blast now does 60 posture damage compared to the previous 36.

- Grants a very large increase to the AoE of your Pressure Blast.
- Careful Stance: [Apprentice Rapier](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Apprentice_Rapier)- Gain a bit more autoparry frames on successful parries.

Clutch Recovery: [Canor Fang](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Canor_Fang)- When at 30% health or below, every time you land a light attack or critical hit heal 1%.

- Landing weapon attacks while your health is at or below 30% will heal you for 1% of your maximum health.

Corporeal Dissolution: [Black Death](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Black_Death)- Applies [Wither](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Wither) on any weapon attack that drains Ether, and scales on the amount of Ether drain.

- This is capped at 30 Wither per proc.

Cruel Counter: [Krulian Knife](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Krulian_Knife)- When using your initial charge up on your Krulian Knife, if you interrupt someone's attack with it apply Cruel Counter on them, massively increasing the damage they take from your critical attacks.

This grants a +40% damage modifier to all critical attacks, even if it isn't a Krulian Knife critical, and even if the critical they got hit by was used by someone else.

- This damage modifier bypasses the damage modifier cap, potentially allowing for absurd critical damage output.

- Curved Edge: [Nemit's Sickle](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Nemit%27s_Sickle)- Your [light weapon mantras](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Attunement-less#Light_Weapon_Mantras) now gain 60% more chip.

Expeditionary Evasiveness: [Inquisitor's Thorn](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Inquisitor%27s_Thorn)- The design of this rapier allows you to much more swiftly attack in tandem with your mantras, giving Agility mantras you use +3 levels in all stats, even bypassing the level cap.

- [Agility Mantras](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Attunement-less#Agility_Mantras) gain +3 Mantra levels. This bypasses the normal Mantra level cap of 5.
- [Jetstriker](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Jetstriker) Mantras are unaffected.

Expeditious Combo: [Iron Birch](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Iron_Birch)- Landing an uppercut will grant a buff to your [Onslaught](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Onslaught) for 15 seconds, using Onslaught during this time will massively increase the speed of it.

- Passively increases the speed of your Onslaught.
- Grants the [Expeditious Combo](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Expeditious_Combo) status effect on uppercut. This status effect will be consumed if you cast Onslaught. The duration of your Expeditious Combo is displayed on the bottom left of the screen on the cooldown indicator.
- This effect has no cooldown, but as the Expeditious Combo status effect is consumed on proc, you will need to land another uppercut to proc it again.
- "Uppercut" or "uptilt" Mantras do not grant the Expeditious Combo status effect; you need to land a basic attack uppercut to proc this.
- Has a sound effect on uppercut.

Extended Trauma: [Morning Star](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Morning_Star)- Successful light attacks and criticals on your opponent now also gives them a bit of posture.

- Your M1s deal 3 posture damage to non-blocking opponents. Your criticals deal 10 posture damage to non-blocking opponents.
- This cannot guardbreak.
- This does not benefit from posture damage modifiers. Additionally, your weapon's damage and weight stat have no impact on this Talent's effects.

Finishing Slay: [Messer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Messer)- If you land a critical attack while also having stacks from the Chain of Perfection talent, consume all those for a massive damaging attack. This works on other players as well.

- Consumes all of your Chains of Perfection stacks for a secondary instance of damage on your critical.
- This deals 7 typeless damage per Chain of Perfection stack. Deals 166 typeless damage per Chain of Perfection stack in PvE.
- The first 5 hits that normally do not grant stacks will contribute to this, allowing you to deal 150 typeless PvP damage or 3.5k typeless PvE damage at maximum chain stacks.
- The damage this deals can be increased with damage modifiers, though it is not considered weapon damage so it is fairly difficult to buff.
- Finishing Slay has no cooldown, allowing for multihit criticals to benefit from it multiple times. However, finishing slay can only proc once per damage instance in a critical hit, for example if you hit three targets with your critical, Finishing Slay will only proc on one of them, and the other two will not take any additional damage.
- This procs on [Motif](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Motif) and [enchant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Enchantments)-granted criticals.

Forced Fulfilment: [Metal Greatsword](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Metal_Greatsword)- Landing a critical attack using your Metal Greatsword attaches a heavy rod onto your opponent, heavily slowing them.

This Talent has no cooldown, allowing you to apply multiple rods to a singular person.

- If a player has two or more Forced Fulfilment rods, they will not be able to move unless they have a speed boost.

Fortify: [Rimebreakers](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rimebreakers)& [Frost Gauntlets](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Frost_Gauntlets)- Slow down your movement in exchange for fortifying your defenses.

- Grants a Talent tool that grants the [Fortified](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Fortified) status effect, granting 15% damage reduction at the cost of reducing your movement speed by 36%.

- Phantom: [Withered Phantomcleave](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Withered_Phantomcleave)- For the next 5 seconds, your next basic attack bypasses block after landing a crit. [45 second CD]

Pierce Through: [Ritual Spear](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ritual_Spear)- Remove the autoparry frames the first hit of your base spear critical has.

- The default spear critical no longer gets canceled if the first hit is parried. Additionally, parrying the first hit of this critical does not provide autoparry frames.

Pugnacious: [Warmonger](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Warmonger)- Become stronger the more you are combat tagged. Gain even more strength whenever you grip a player.

On basic attack, gain +10% to the following stats: damage, posture damage, elemental damage resistance, physical damage resistance, chip damage, and speed boost per combat tag you have.

- The damage, posture damage, and chip damage bonuses are additive and ignore the damage and posture damage modifier caps. These effects cap at +30% (3 or more combat tags), and are applied to Mantras as well. The chip damage bonus will only apply if some amount of chip is already present.
- The elemental and physical damage resistance bonuses are multiplicative to other resistance sources.
- The speed boost has no cap and is applied per M1. For example, if you land 5 m1s and are combat tagged by 5 people, you will gain +250% speed. Each speed boost stack lasts 5 seconds and does not refresh upon acquiring another. Additionally, being hit by an attack will also proc this bonus.

Punishing Riposte: [Crucible Rapier](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Crucible_Rapier)- When parrying a guardbreak, enhance your critical for the next 15 seconds. If you land your crit on your opponent's block while it is enhanced, immediately guardbreak your opponent.

- After parrying a guard break, gain the [Punishing Riposte](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Punishing_Riposte) status effect for 15 seconds, making your critical guaranteed guardbreak if it hits block.

Rat's Spirit: [Champion's Dagger](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Champion%27s_Dagger)- Each hit of your critical will deal an extra 10 true damage per combat tag whenever you are combat tagged by 2 or more people.

- Being combat tagged by 2 or more players will cause your critical attack to deal an additional 10 true damage per hit. This stacks based on how many combat tags you have, dealing a minimum of +20 true damage per hit.

Rosen's Technique: [Rosen's Peacemaker](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rosen%27s_Peacemaker)- If you use Tactical Reload, Rosen's Peacemaker has 45 seconds less off its cooldown.

- Reduces the cooldown of Tactical Reload by 45 seconds; from 90 seconds to 45.

Sable Winds: [Eyes of Ethiron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Eyes_of_Ethiron)- You can now Inhale Shadowcast mantras. Aftercut effects now drain a small amount of ether based on the damage they deal.

- Your Shadowcast Mantras now gain the damage buff and the modification transfer effect from Inhale. Additionally, you can also Inhale Shadowcast Mantras.

Sacrificial Boon: [Ritual Sacrifice](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ritual_Sacrifice)- Sacrifice 5 knowledge to activate Blessing, giving you a random blessing from Navae.

The potential buffs are:

- 20% damage buff. This is also applied to Mantras
- Hyperarmor for 10 seconds
- Instantly heal 50 health
- Thirst restoration
- Stomach restoration
- Speed boost for 10 seconds

Soar: [Withered Gale Pale](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Withered_Gale_Pale)- Retain full walkspeed while using your crit.

- Using your critical attack will no longer slow you.
- This Talent allows you to retain all of your momentum during your weapon's critical attack, making it extremely strong for playstyles that utilize sliding and Gale/Phantom Step dashes often.
- Equipping a [Motif](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Motif) onto the Withered Gale Pale will disable this Talent.

Sovereign's Counter: [Inquisitor's Greatsword](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Inquisitor%27s_Greatsword)- Improve the efficiency of every counter you use. This effect becomes stronger for Punishment if you have the Riot Breaker talent.

[Punishment](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Punishment) has its reflected damage output increased by 12.5%, meaning at level 5 you can reflect 112.5% of someone's damage on top of the 30 base damage. The base damage of this Mantra is unaffected.

- If you are wearing the [Authority Commander](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Authority_Commander) Outfit, the longevity of the attack window on your punishment is increased by 3x; from 20 seconds to a full minute.

- Oath-based counters ([Illusory Counter](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Illusory_Counter) and [Equalizer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Equalizer)) do not have any affect with this Talent.
- Spinal Splinter: [Halberd](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Halberd)- Landing a backstab with your critical deals 15 extra true damage.

Static Beatdown: [Legion Cestus](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Legion_Cestus)- The metal the cestus is made from allows electricity to be conducted easily through it, allowing you to further extend how long your opponents are stuck in shaky block based on your total Thundercall investment.

- Increases shaky block duration by 0.004s per point in Thundercall. At 100 Thundercall, shaky block lasts 1 second against players with 1x or slower swing speed weapons.

- Subzero: [Winter Rifle](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Winter_Rifle)- Landing a running attack inflicts your opponent with a deep freeze. Additional light hits after the opponent thaws out will briefly slow them as well.

Swift Strike: [Big Brother](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Big_Brother)- Land a light attack behind your opponent to enhance your next critical, allowing you to teleport behind your opponent and strike them. This enhanced critical also procs assassination talents.

- On proc, you gain the Swift Strike status effect indefinitely. This effect will be consumed when you use your critical.
- The teleport has a range limit.
- 20 second cooldown.

True Seraph Slash: [True Seraph's Spear](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Seraph%27s_Spear)- Landing the first hit of your critical attack enhances your next critical, giving you access to the Angels' spear smite ability.

- This deals 0.5x your weapons scaled damage per hit (4), dealt as typeless damage.

True Shatter: [Coldpoint](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Coldpoint)- Landing a critical shatters your opponent, ridding them of their speed debuffs and dealing true damage scaling off how slow they were.

- Deals 5 true damage per slow effect removed.

Vital Wound: [Acheron's Warspear](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Acheron%27s_Warspear)- Uppercutting your opponent punctures their lungs, slowing their rate of posture recovery for 30 seconds.

- Uppercutting your opponent applies the [Vital Wound](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Vital_Wound) status effect, reducing their passive posture recovery by 40% for 30 seconds.
- This has no cooldown, but cannot be reapplied to the same target once active.

## Equipment Talents

Certain Talents are given when a specific equipment or outfit is worn. Multiple copies of the same Talent do not stack. Equipment Talents are listed in alphabetical order:

Actions Speak Louder: [Warmaster's Medallion](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Warmaster%27s_Medallion)- Your Critical Attack cooldown is 20% shorter, but your Resonance cooldown is 20% longer. In areas where your Resonance is suppressed, reduce your cooldown by 10% instead.

- Your critical cooldown is reduced by 20%, but your Resonance cooldown is 20% longer.
- In 1v1 [Chime of Conflict](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Chime_of_Conflict), your critical cooldown is reduced by 10% instead.
- This effect is applied before Critical Engine Blessed Gem is applied, allowing for 60% reduction in your critical cooldown (55% in Chime of Conflict).

- Alloyed Soles: [Ossified Phalanx Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ossified_Phalanx_Boots)& [Alloyed Phalanx Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Alloyed_Phalanx_Boots)- Reduces the duration of [Knockdown](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Knockdown) applied to you.

Already Dead: [Deepscorn Casque](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Deepscorn_Casque)- You take reduced damage from abilities with a health cost.

Reduces the self damage from [Shade Devour](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shade_Devour), Flame Within (on cast, NOT the burn ticks), Rush Hour, Shadow Travel, and Electrify.

- When paired with the [Stormchanter's Raiments](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Stormchanter%27s_Raiments) Talent, Circuit Breaker, using Electrify will cause you to heal instead of taking damage.

Angel's Guise: [Hollow Angel Mask](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Hollow_Angel_Mask)& [Sworn Angel Mask](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Sworn_Angel_Mask)- You resemble one of the Sworn Angels.

- Grants immunity to the [Watcher](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Watcher)'s Watcher Gaze attack, preventing Sanity loss, vision distortion, and the spawning of [Hollow Angel](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Hollow_Angel)(s).
- Grants unique dialogue with [Watchers](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Watcher), as you cannot speak to Watchers without this Talent.
- Angels will still aggro onto you while you have this Talent.

Bane: [Familiar Assassin's Armor](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Familiar_Assassin%27s_Armor)& [Hidden Knife Armor](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Hidden_Knife_Armor)- Activate to make your light attacks teleport to the closest enemy nearby. [100s CD]

- Grants a Talent tool that on use allows you to teleport to your opponent when you M1 for 20 seconds.
- The teleportation has a range limit of 25 studs.
- Bane additionally grants a speed boost for its full duration.

- Battle-Hardened: [Shock Corps Light](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shock_Corps_Light)- Reduce how much damage you take from mantra guardbreaks by 20%.

Benefactor: [Varicosan Finery](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Varicosan_Finery)- Gain reputation for selling goods to Antiquarians within faction territories.

- Grants faction [reputation](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Reputation) when selling items to their respective Antiquarian.

Blinded: [Blindfold](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Blindfold)& Variants - Your vision is obscured by something. Somehow, you feel safer. You remember the warmth of your youth.

- Makes everything darker and creates fog at long distances
- Even though the [Blindseer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Blindseer) Oath grants full vision even with a Blindfold, the Talent still works.
- The Talent grants immunity to the [Flame Blind](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Flame_Blind) and [Gaze](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Gaze) Mantras.

- Blood Convergence: [Regenerative Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Regenerative_Earrings)& [Trueblood Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Trueblood_Earrings)- Receive 10% more healing from all healing sources when you have [temp health](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Temporary_Health).

Blood Necrosis: [The No-Life King's Crown](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/The_No-Life_King%27s_Crown)- Your [bloodless gems](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Bloodless_Gem) heal 33% less than usual.

- You will now need a scaled damage of 94 (up from 63) to heal 15 health, and a scaled damage of 187 to reach 30 healing.

- Blood Pact: [Regenerative Pendant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Regenerative_Pendant)& [Trueblood Pendant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Trueblood_Pendant)- Gain 50 bonus [temporary health](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Temporary_Health) whenever you knock/kill an enemy.

Blood Plague: [Necrotic Mask](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Necrotic_Mask)& [Bloodcurdle Mask](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Bloodcurdle_Mask)- For every light attack you land, add one Blood Plague stack on your opponent. Landing a Critical Attack converts all Blood Plague stacks into temporary health but missing the attack loses them.

- M1s apply stacks of [Blood Plague](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Blood_Plague).
- On critical, all Blood Plague stacks will be consumed to grant [Temporary Health](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Temporary_Health) if your critical attack lands, with the amount of Temporary Health given scaling on the stacks of Blood Plague.
- The stacks of Blood Plague will be consumed regardless of if your critical attack lands, however.

- Blood Siphon: [Necrotic Cowl](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Necrotic_Cowl)& [Bloodcurdle Cowl](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Bloodcurdle_Cowl)- Your Critical Attack now siphons a bit of health, healing you 10% of the damage you dealt.

Breeze: [Stratos' Cloak](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Stratos%27_Cloak)- Gain a permanent +2 speed buff at all times.

- Increases your movement speed by +2 studs/s, permanently.

Brunt: [Hive Tactician Armor](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Hive_Tactician_Armor)- While 2-handing, you take less posture damage.

- Reduce posture damage taken by 10% while 2 handing a weapon (unavailable for light weapons).

Brute Strength: [Imperator's Fury](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Imperator%27s_Fury)- Your vent is replaced with a ground slam attack that knocks back enemies.

- Deals 35 Blunt damage with 20 Posture damage.
- Cannot be buffed through damage modifiers.
- Increases your Vent's windup from 0.25s to 0.5s.
- Procs all "on Vent" Talents and effects.

Centurion's Resolve: [Legion Centurion](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Legion_Centurion)- Your [Legion Kata](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Legion_Kata)/ [Imperium Kata](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Imperium_Kata) attacks deal 4% more damage.

- Counts as a damage modifier, so it will build up to and is affected by the damage modifier cap.

Chief's Will: [Navaen War Chief](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Navaen_War_Chief)- [Way of Navae](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Way_of_Navae) light attacks have 10% additional penetration.

- Gives [Way of Navae](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Way_of_Navae) M1s 10% more PEN. This does NOT apply to criticals unless it has the [M1 tag](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/M1_tag).
- This bypasses the 50% PEN cap.

Circuit Breaker: [Stormchanter's Raiments](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Stormchanter%27s_Raiments)- Electrify no longer deals self-damage on use.

- The Electrify Talent tool no longer consumes health on use.
- When paired with the [Deepscorn Casque](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Deepscorn_Casque) Talent, Already Dead, using Electrify will cause you to heal instead.

Coldseep Reactor: [Grand Fisher Helm](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Grand_Fisher_Helm)- By cultivating the localized chemosynthetic microorganisms within the Depths and utilizing them as a power source, your helm thrums with a protective field of static electricity and heat.

- Grants full immunity to the [Second Layer's](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Second_Layer) Parasites.

Corrosive Touch: [Arachnid's Weave](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Arachnid%27s_Weave)- Landing a successful Critical Attack or Flourish will corrode a portion of your opponent's Armor.

- Drain 5% of your opponent's armor after landing a Critical attack or Flourish. This has a 10 second cooldown and can only proc once per critical attack.

Cosmic Connection: [Celestial Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Celestial_Boots)- All celestial and astral related abilities are buffed.

- Increases your [Astral](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Astral) enchant damage bonus on basic attacks from +20% to +25%.

Crippling Darkness: [Prophet's Cloak](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Prophet%27s_Cloak)& [Moonseye](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Moonseye_(Outfit))- Your non-Basic Attacks have 20% PEN.

Grants +20% PEN to anything that is not a weapon attack.

- Primarily, this is just 20% Mantra PEN, however this will also affect non-Mantra/weapon damage sources like Crystal detonations, Eruptions, Surge overloads, and others.
- [Reinforce's](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Reinforce) general resistance is affected by PEN, meaning Crippling Darkness will make things such as bleed or Steam, which bypass armor, deal more damage against Reinforce than they would otherwise.

Crippling Impact: [Reinforced War Plate](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Reinforced_War_Plate)- Landing a critical with someone while [Wrath Gem](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Wrath_Gem) is active disables their vent for a few seconds.

- Procs through parry, block and dodge with no cooldown.
- Duration is 5 seconds + 0.02 seconds per point of Strength, lasting 7 seconds at 100 Strength.
- Wrath Gems last 9 seconds with no visual indication.

Demon Step: [Familiar Demon's Armor](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Familiar_Demon%27s_Armor)- Activate to gain brief iframes while moving forward. This requires 50 ether to activate and increases your posture by 20% each use.

- On use, lose 50 Ether to travel a short distance forwards in iframes. Additionally, 20% of your current posture will be dealt to you as self-posture damage.
- 1 second cooldown.

Destructive Yell: [Ascended Outlaw Mask](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ascended_Outlaw_Mask)- Your Ardour Screams now break campfires around you, while increasing its range by 2x.

- Your Ardour Scream now destroys all campfires within its range.
- Also doubles the range of your Ardour Scream.

Devastating Power: [Pathfinder Arch-Sorcerer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Pathfinder_Arch-Sorcerer)- Gain extra Ether for every Mantra in your arsenal.

Maximum of +140 Ether (6 innate Mantra slots, +1 from Neuroplasticity, +1 from Will o' Wisp, +1 from Wisp Convergence, +5 from [Saintsworn](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Saintsworn)).

- Wisp Convergence will not work if you do not have Oathless, but it will still grant its additional Wisp Mantra slot.

Diver's Resilience: [Grand Fisher Plate](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Grand_Fisher_Plate)- You can parry unparryable attacks from giant monsters, but due to the heft of the plate, you have slightly reduced speed.

- 20 second cooldown.
- Cannot parry ALL types of attacks, similar to [Hunter's Brace](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Hunter%27s_Brace), you can parry kicks and some other knockback moves. Additionally, your movement speed is reduced.

- Drop Dead: [Vaporfrost Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vaporfrost_Earrings)& [Vapordrop Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vapordrop_Earrings)- Take less damage when Crouching.

Elegy of Light: [Lightkeeper's Medallion](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Lightkeeper%27s_Medallion)- The Unspoken Vow of the Waking God resonates through you, if briefly. Protects you from the effects of Deep Gems for 3 minutes and provides you [Gem Enhancement](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Gem_Enhancement). Remains dormant until you take the life of an equal.

- Grants a Talent tool ability. Upon activating it, your body will emit light and Deep Gems cannot be procced on you. This effect lasts 3 minutes.
- Gem Enhancement increases the effectiveness of your Deep Gems in PvE.
- Once the duration ends, you will need to kill another player of an equal power or a boss to recharge it.

Enforcer's Strength: [Hardened Enforcer Plate](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Hardened_Enforcer_Plate)& [Reinforced Enforcer Plate](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Reinforced_Enforcer_Plate)- Your enemies recover 20% less posture on parry.

- Enemies recover 20% less posture when they parry your attacks.

- Enforcer's Technique: [Hardened Enforcer Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Hardened_Enforcer_Boots)& [Reinforced Enforcer Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Reinforced_Enforcer_Boots)- Your flourishes deal 35% more damage.

Ether Adeptness: [Ether Empowered Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ether_Empowered_Earrings)& [Caster Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Caster_Earrings)- Your mantras now deal extra chip.

- Grants 5% Mantra chip.

Ether Emergency: [Bluestone Pauldrons](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Bluestone_Pauldrons)- When you get hit below 25% health, exhaust all of your ether to gain a flat health boost (healing scales of total max ether). You are briefly unable to cast Mantras. 5m CD

- Instantly restores 15% of your maximum Ether as flat health at the cost of all of your Ether and the inability to cast Mantras for a short duration.

Ether Pinpoint: [Ascended Outlaw Robes](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ascended_Outlaw_Robes)- Mantras that have [Might Gem](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Might_Gem) on them now ignore fully ignore the posture bonus from shields.

- Acts similarly to Shield Breaker, though this does not require the Mantra to deal blunt damage.

Ether Tension: [Ether Imbued Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ether_Imbued_Earrings)& [Expert Practitioner's Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Expert_Practitioner%27s_Earrings)- Deal bonus true damage whenever you guardbreak an opponent with a mantra.

- Deals 10 true damage.

Fatal Strike: [Reinforced War Helmet](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Reinforced_War_Helmet)- Landing a guard break with a strength mantra that has a [Wrath Gem](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Wrath_Gem) on it devastates your opponent, dealing extra damage and slowing them down.

- Adds a +20% damage modifier to your [Strength Mantras](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Attunement-less#Strength_Mantras), should they guardbreak while you have a [Wrath Gem](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Wrath_Gem) equipped onto them.
- Applies a 50% slow for 5.5 seconds on proc.
- 10 second cooldown.

Featherfall: [Tiran Pendant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Tiran_Pendant)- Prevents falls from damaging you. After sufficient damage has been resisted, the pendant will go inactive and require time to recharge.

- Negates up to 255 fall damage before going on a 2 minute cooldown.
- The fall damage negated, before this goes on cooldown, does not reset and saves between servers.

- Ferryman's Curse: [Ferryman's Coat](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ferryman%27s_Coat)- Wearing this gives you a 20% chance to convert incoming elemental damage into Lightning damage.

Fists of Navae: [Navaen Nomad Robes](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Navaen_Nomad_Robes)- Double the amount of ether you gain on successful light attacks.

- Doubles the Ether gained from landed M1 attacks.

Flashwind: [Specialist Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Specialist_Boots)- Anytime you proc Air Pressure or Overcharge, give yourself the ability to have enhanced dashes again for a few seconds.

- After using a Lightning or Gale dash, all dashes within the next 3 seconds will become enhanced. These enhanced dashes have the same effectiveness as Lightning and Gale dashes.
- While similar to Lightning/Gale dashes, this is its own thing and ignores their cooldowns.

- Fleetfoot: [Carefree Garments](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Carefree_Garments)- Gain an initial speed boost when slide jumping.

- Focused Hematoma: [Sanguine Finery](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Sanguine_Finery)- Decrease outside sources of blood loss by 25% but reduce the amount of temporary health you receive by 15%.

- Focused Strikes: [Gale Enhanced Beads](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Gale_Enhanced_Beads)& [Hallowed Monastery Beads](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Hallowed_Monastery_Beads)- All criticals deal 15% more posture damage.

Footwork Mastery: [Gale Enhanced Cowl](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Gale_Enhanced_Cowl)& [Hallowed Monastery Cowl](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Hallowed_Monastery_Cowl)- Landing a critical gives you an immense speed boost for 10 seconds.

- 45 second cooldown.
- The speed boost lasts 15 seconds despite what the description states.

Force Your Way: [Ignition Deepdelver](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ignition_Deepdelver), [Ignition Gauntlets](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ignition_Gauntlets)& [Reforged Gauntlets](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Reforged_Gauntlets)- You can parry unparryable attacks from giant monsters, at the cost of armor durability.

- This does not allow you to parry all unparryable monster attacks.
- The armor durability lost is based on the amount of damage you would have taken normally.
- When parrying attacks that are slide, jump, or slide only, the armor loss penalty will be greatly increased.

Grotesque Resilience: [Parasol Planter](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Parasol_Planter)- You take reduced damage from 'Damage over Time' effects.

Reduces Burn damage by 50%

- If you are utilizing Flame Within, the DoT damage reduction will be reduced down to 25%.

Does not work on all Damage over Time effects.

- Does not work on: Poison (hp drain) [Corrupt Resonance](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Resonance#Corrupted_Resonances) Downside, [Deep Widow](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Deep_Widow) Poison, [Bladeharper's](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Oath:_Bladeharper) Reveal, [Viscosity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Viscosity), or Rush Hour from Bloodrend.

Heartwing Beat: [Mantle of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Mantle_of_Enmity)- Your heart beats a new rhythm, as your aerial attack takes on a new manifestation.

Heartwing Beat has 25 base damage, and mirrors the scaling stat from your equipped weapon. Tangentially, this Talent uses the weapon scaling formula.

- For example, using Heartwing Beat on the [Sword](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Sword) will give it 2.5 Medium scaling, while using it on the [Repeater](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Repeater) will give it 15 Light Weapon scaling.

- Heartwing Beat has a 10 second cooldown.
- Herbivore: [Herbalist's Hat](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Herbalist%27s_Hat) and [Big Herbalist's Hat](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Big_Herbalist%27s_Hat)- You gain more nutrition from eating plants.

Hunter's Reflexes: [Cutthroat Light Armor](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Cutthroat_Light_Armor)- You have a slightly larger dodge window.

- Your dodge window is increased by 0.05s.

Hyperbody: [Familiar Knight's Armor](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Familiar_Knight%27s_Armor)- Apply a buff to your allies around you that increases your speed, defense, and regeneration. This effect becomes weaker the more allies you buff with it. [2 min CD]

Increases your movement speed by 7.5 studs/s. This speed boost will halve in effectiveness for each ally buffed.

- For example, at one ally you'll only gain +3.75 studs/s movement speed, at two allies this'll be reduced further to +1.875 studs per second, and so on.
- This counts as a speed boost for Talent effects.

Grants a 35.5% damage reduction buff. This is increased by 20% multiplicatively for every ally buffed.

- For example at one ally, this will grant +42.6% damage reduction, at two allies this will grant 51.12% damage reduction, and so on.

I'm Blue: [Bluestone Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Bluestone_Boots)- Improve the efficiency of your [Blue Gems](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Blue_Gem). Blue are the gems you use.

- Increases the effectiveness of Blue Gems by 20%, additively.
- With I'm Blue, Blue Gems reduce the total Ether cost of Mantras by 45%.

Immortality: [Immortal Helm](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Immortal_Helm)- The remnants of the helmet's Mind Veil stir into life when you would be made [Unconscious](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Unconscious) soaking all damage for a brief duration. 30s CD. [Viscosity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Viscosity) is rejected.

- Grants one instance of knock prevention, leaving you at 1% HP on proc.
- The [Viscosity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Viscosity) enchantment will be disabled while wearing this equipment.

Inky Pearls: [Dark Pearls](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dark_Pearls)- The ether you eject is coated with inky shadow. Your vent now steals a small amount of ether.

- Turns your Vent black and makes it drain 47.5 Ether on hit.
- Increases your Vent's Ether cost on successful hit from 22.5 to 47.5.
- Your Vent can proc Shadowcast Talents on hit such as Twisted Puppet's Shadowcast flames and Sightless Still.

- Instant Nucleation: [Frost Crystal Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Frost_Crystal_Earrings)& [Hardened Crystal Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Hardened_Crystal_Earrings)- Take less damage for a few seconds after you are guardbroken.

Intrepid Flame: [Flame Worshipper Armor](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Flame_Worshipper_Armor)- Flames wear off twice as fast on you.

- Halves the maximum duration of any Burn that is applied to you.
- Does not work on Flame Within.

Jester's Ruse: [Jester's Beret](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Jester%27s_Beret)& [Trickster's Beret](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Trickster%27s_Beret)- Anytime you take damage, there's a 10% chance it gets completely nullified. Anytime you deal damage, there's a 10% chance it also gets completely nullified.

- [Visionshaper](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Visionshaper)'s Cheap Trick visual effect plays on proc.
- This can proc if an attack is blocked, dodged, or parried. When this happens, the visual effect will play, but nothing will happen.

Knack: [Eager Tradesman](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Eager_Tradesman)- Trees yield more Wood when felled. Your Repair speed is increased.

- Increases the boat repair speed.

Laminated Armor: [Ossified Black Pauldrons](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ossified_Black_Pauldrons)& [Blackalloy Pauldrons](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Blackalloy_Pauldrons)- Reduces all incoming damage by 2/3 (applied after damage multipliers).

- Reduces damage taken by a flat 2 (3 in Vow of Iron), not 66%.
- This is applied after all other calculations, allowing you to reduce an attack's damage to 0, however it cannot reduce an attack's damage below 0.

- Legendary: [Grand Pathfinder Cloak](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Grand_Pathfinder_Cloak)- Parrying and landing hits with your weapon gives you ether back.

- Lethal Dose: [Inquisition Light](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Inquisition_Light)- The poison from your [hidden blade](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Hidden_Blade) lasts twice as long.

Life Leech: [Familiar Occultist's Armor](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Familiar_Occultist%27s_Armor)- Your summons now heal you each time they deal damage.

Eligible summons include:

- Monsters summoned from the [Parasol's Blight](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Parasol%27s_Blight) enchantment
- Spiderlings summoned from [The Weaving Web](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/The_Weaving_Web)'s critical attack
- [Visionshaper](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Visionshaper) clones from [Illusory Servants](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Illusory_Servants) and [Illusory Counter](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Illusory_Counter)
- [Lightning Clones](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Lightning_Clones)
- [Flame Sentinel](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Flame_Sentinel)

Ineligible summons include:

- [Metal Turret](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Metal_Turret)
- [Visionshaper](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Visionshaper) clones from [Mirror Illusion](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Mirror_Illusion)
- You'll Need to Get Past Me Talent
- Twisted Puppets' puppets

Looter: [Experienced Adventurer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Experienced_Adventurer)- Get extra loot from items you turn in for rewards.

This affects:

- Turning in [Artifacts](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Artifacts) at a banker,
- Turning in the [Strange Egg](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Artifacts#Strange_Egg) to the banker at [Greathive Aratel](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Greathive_Aratel),
- Turning in [Sacks](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Chests_%26_Sacks#Sacks) to Mercille at the summer company outpost across from [Meteor Isle](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Meteor_Isle),
- Handing in pure ore to a [Blacksmith](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Blacksmith),
- Trading in Explosive Crates at [Summer Isle](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Summer_Isle).

Mass Effect: [Ascended Outlaw Hat](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ascended_Outlaw_Hat)- For every [Might Gem](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Might_Gem) you have equipped on your mantras, slightly increase each Might Gem's power.

- Increases the posture damage bonus Might Gems innately grant, scaling on the amount of Might Gems you have equipped in your arsenal.
- Grants your Might Gems an additional 2.25% posture damage multiplier per Mantra with a Might Gem equipped. This caps at +39.25% posture damage dealt on a Might Gem-equipped Mantra, assuming you have [Saintsworn](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Saintsworn), Neuroplasticity, and Will o' Wisp with all 13 of your equipped Mantras using a Might Gem.

Merchant's Accord: [Merchant Robes](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Merchant_Robes)- Merchants and Antiquarians will do business with you no matter how poor your reputation is.

- This Talent does NOT allow you to use the Antiquarian in the [First Layer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/First_Layer) if you are not [Deepbound](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Deepbound) or [Ignition Delver](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ignition_Delver).

Mindspark: [Necrotic Demon Horns](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Necrotic_Demon_Horns)& [Flame Demon Horns](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Flame_Demon_Horns)- The damage of your flames scale with how insane you are.

- Your burn damage is increased based on how low your [Sanity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Sanity) is.
- Grants +0.0205 flat damage to each burn tick, for every 1% Sanity you've lost, capping at +2.05 damage at 0% Sanity.

Mocking Favor: [Jester's Garb](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Jester%27s_Garb)& [Trickster's Habit](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Trickster%27s_Habit)- When taunting, Aelita or Tillian appears, bestowing upon you a random boon of affliction. (30s cooldown)

List of buffs being:

- [Visionshaper](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Visionshaper) invisibility from the Cheap Trick Talent for a short duration.
- Grants you ~18% Tempo. Indicated by a light blue effect, somewhat similar to [Reinforce](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Reinforce)'s vfx.
- 30% Posture restoration. Indicated by a metal clanging sound effect.
- A free reroll, granting you an additional buff or affliction.
- One 0 cost Mantra, this affects your allies as well. Indicated by a dark blue [Reinforce](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Reinforce)-like vfx.
- A percentage of the damage you've taken recently will be turned into healing.
- Vent cooldown reset, allowing you to Vent again.

List of afflictions being:

- Grants a random spit cooldown. This can be anywhere between 5 and 20 seconds.
- Suffocation applied to self for a few seconds.
- Lowers your Swingspeed. Indicated by black 'x' marks coming off of your body.

No Interruptions: [Champion's Cape](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Champion%27s_Cape)- When combat tagged by 2 or more players, render any ranged attack a lot less effective.

- This grants +25% damage reduction against attacks from opponents who are at least 15 studs away from you. This damage reduction is increased by 1% per additional stud of distance.
- Grants [true hyperarmor](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#True_Hyperarmor) against attacks from opponents who are at least 15 studs away from you.
- Despite what the description states, this Talent can activate if you have one PvE combat tag and one PvP combat tag.

No Limits: [The No-Life King's Crown](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/The_No-Life_King%27s_Crown)& [Infused Bloodforged Crown](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Infused_Bloodforged_Crown)- Increase the cap on [Bloodless Gem](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Bloodless_Gem) healing.

- The Bloodless Gem heal cap is increased from 15 to 30.
- You will need a scaled damage of 187 (in Pathfinder) or 125 (in [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron)) to reach this new heal cap.

Overwhelming Might: [Titus's Raiment](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Titus%27s_Raiment)- [Strong Left](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Strong_Left) now guard breaks at level 5.

- 1 minute cooldown.

Paired Soul: [Vigil Initiate](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vigil_Initiate), [Cloak of Winds](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Cloak_of_Winds)& [Royal Etrean Guard](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Royal_Etrean_Guard)- Wielding a katana with this outfit grants extra damage.

- Increases [Katana](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Katana) damage by 25%.
- Increases [Shattered Katana](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Katana), [Alloyed Katana](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Alloyed_Katana) and [Purple Cloud](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Purple_Cloud) damage by 5%.
- Counts as a damage modifier, so it will build up to and is affected by the damage modifier cap.

- Praise The Sun: [Revitalizing Pendant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Revitalizing_Pendant)& [Solar Pendant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Solar_Pendant)- When standing in sunlight increase your natural health regen and reduce how long antiheal effects last on you.

Primal Rage: [Titanslayer's Adornment](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Titanslayer%27s_Adornment)- If you are below 50%, unleash your rage and increase the amount of damage you deal for 25 seconds as well as being able to see enemy health when you hit them. [3 minute CD]

- Grants a Talent tool that can only be used while under 50% health.
- Grants a 25% damage buff for the duration of the buff.
- The health highlight on hit effect is very similar to [Soulbreaker's](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Oath:_Soulbreaker) Heart Reverb Talent.

- Refreeze: [Icebringer's Vestments](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Icebringer%27s_Vestments)- Landing any sort of ability that causes bottom freeze reduces the cooldown of your Orbital Ice by 8 seconds.

Repeated Blows: [Reinforced War Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Reinforced_War_Boots)- Any [strength](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Attunement-less#Strength) mantra that has a [Wrath Gem](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Wrath_Gem) on it will reset its cooldown if you land a critical while a Wrath Gem is active.

- Wrath Gems are active for 9 seconds with no visual indication.

Repeated Propulsion: [Cindergarb](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Cindergarb)- Reduce the cooldown of Dancing Steps and Graceful Steps to 1 second.

- Reduces the cooldown of Dancing Steps and Graceful Steps from 5 seconds to 1.

- Riot Breaker: [Authority Commander](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Authority_Commander)- You receive 15% less posture damage and deal 10% more posture damage when under attack by 3 or more enemies.

Rock Blaster: [Miner's Hardhat](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Miner%27s_Hardhat)- Doubles your ore yields when mining.

- Combining this Talent with excavator gives you a chance to get 4 ores at a time.

- Scholar's Intuition: [Scholar](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Scholar)- INT, WILL, and CHA Training Gear is 50% more effective.

Seamaster's Guile: [Seamaster's Cap](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Seamaster%27s_Cap)- While you're at the helm of a ship, it takes 25% less damage

- Your boat will take 25% less damage if the person piloting it has this Talent.

Serpent's Dance: [Jade Vigil's Weave](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Jade_Vigil%27s_Weave)- Unleash the power of Fang and Coil to unlock a devastating Running Critical Attack, feint to cancel momentum.

- Allows you to use [Fang and Coil](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Fang_and_Coil)'s running critical. This attack does not work on any Cestus that already has a unique crit.

Sharpened Dagger: [Ossified Blood Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ossified_Blood_Earrings)& [Assassin's Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Assassin%27s_Earrings)- All backstabs deal 1 extra true damage (4 extra damage if done to PvE).

- This applies to attacks used against your opponent's back, not assassinations.

Simmerbloom: [Simmerbloom Diadem](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Simmerbloom_Diadem)- Basic Attacks on [Pleeksty's Inferno](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Pleeksty%27s_Inferno) are now granted flames.

[Pleeksty's Inferno](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Pleeksty%27s_Inferno) Basic Attacks now apply burn/eruptions. This has no cooldown.

- Due to this equipment Talent, Pleeksty Inferno users can save a few, otherwise necessary, Talents like Flaming Flourish.

Simple Buff: [Ossified Simple Pauldrons](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ossified_Simple_Pauldrons)& [Super Simple Pauldrons](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Super_Simple_Pauldrons)- Your light attacks deal 7.5/15% more damage. Pretty simple.

- Grants a 7.5% damage buff to M1s (15% in Vow of Iron).

- Skillful Recovery: [Shiva's Pendant Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shiva%27s_Pendant_Earrings)& [Warrior's Pendant Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Warrior%27s_Pendant_Earrings)- Recover more posture in all situations.

- Sloped Plate: [Ossified Black Helm](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ossified_Black_Helm)& [Blackalloy Helm](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Blackalloy_Helm)- Reduces the amount of damage you take when guardbroken by 10.

- Smoldering Touch: [Ember Scorch Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ember_Scorch_Earrings)& [Ruby Scorch Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ruby_Scorch_Earrings)- Guardbreaking your opponent sets them on fire.

- Sorcerer's Skill: [Ether Refined Brim](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ether_Refined_Brim)& [Sorcerer's Brim](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Sorcerer%27s_Brim)- Decrease the amount of [tempo](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Tempo) it takes to [vent](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vent) by 5.

- Sorcerer's Surge: [Ether Refined Mantle](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ether_Refined_Mantle)& [Sorcerer's Mantle](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Sorcerer%27s_Mantle)- Your vent's cooldown is reduced by 2.5 seconds.

Soul Rip: [Familiar Heretic's Armor](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Familiar_Heretic%27s_Armor)- Kill a player to gain 2 runes. Activate a rune when using this talent, and gain immense physical power, defense, and infinite ether for 25 seconds. Beware its strenuous downside.

- Killing a player will give you 2 Runes, and using this Talent's Talent tool will consume 1 Rune, granting you buffs in exchange.
- Increases your weapon damage by 50%, gives ?% damage reduction, and grants infinite Ether for 25 second.
- Once the buff wears off, one of your legs will break, removing your ability to sprint for 30 seconds.
- Though not stated in the Talent's description, this also gives a speed boost for the full effect's duration. You'll still be at a net negative in terms of movement speed, but you'll be able to benefit from speed boost reliant Talents such as Speed Demon.

- Star Duster: [Star Duster](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Star_Duster)- You take 30% less damage from airborne enemies.

Stormcall: [Specialist Plate](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Specialist_Plate)- Anytime you land a storm strike apply stormcall shock on your opponent, damaging them if they try to use a mantra while its active.

- Storm Strikes from other Specialist Equipment Talents now apply the Stormcall Shock status effect, causing your opponent to take damage if they cast Mantras during its duration.
- Stormcall Shock deals 15 Gale and Thunder damage, applies Shock for 1 second or 1 Surge Rod, and winded for 2 seconds upon casting any Mantra.

Stronger Under Pressure: [Ascended Outlaw Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ascended_Outlaw_Boots)- Your mantras with [might gem](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Might_Gem) now slow down your opponents, scaling with how many combat tags you currently have.

- This applies a 5% slow per combat tag you have. This slow lasts 1 second.

- Sunset Ricochet: [Summer Dragoon](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Summer_Dragoon)- Makes your bullets ricochet to a nearby enemy when hitting an opponent.

Surestep: [Delver Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Delver_Boots)& [Marrowmade Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Marrowmade_Boots)- Your boots secure your footing in all forms of terrain by digging into the ground with metal spikes.

- Makes you immune to the ragdoll and movement speed debuff of the Wind from the [Second Layer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Second_Layer) and [Moon's Eyrie](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Moon%27s_Eyrie).

Survivalist: [Pathfinder Cloak](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Pathfinder_Cloak)& [Ranger's Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ranger%27s_Boots)- Resist the effects of Weather Effects.

- Reduces the damage taken from [Acid Rain](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Acid_Rain).

Tempest Evolution: [Tempestmaker's Threads](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Tempestmaker%27s_Threads)- Your slide-jumped [Wind Blades](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Wind_Blade) are naturally stronger and if landed grant +20% damage in the air for a few seconds.

- Grants a 20% damage buff to your slide-jump [Wind Blade](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Wind_Blade). Also increases its range.

- Temple Guard: [Monastery Champion Robes](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Monastery_Champion_Robes)- For the first 0.3s of your Critical Attack's windup, you take 90% less damage from any attacks that cancel your windup.

- Tenacity: [Ossified Barrel Helm](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ossified_Barrel_Helm)& [Hardened Barrel Helm](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Hardened_Barrel_Helm)- Greatly shorten the amount of time you are guardbroken for.

The Path, Unveiled: [Justicar](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Justicar)& [Mod Suit](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Mod_Suit)- Your [Jus Karita](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Jus_Karita) attacks deal 15% more damage.

- Only grants a 10% damage buff, the Talent was nerfed but the description was never changed.
- Counts as a damage modifier, so it will build up to and is affected by the damage modifier cap.

- Tidekeeper: [Celtorian Tideknight](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Celtorian_Tideknight)- Losing 25% of your health within the span of 3 seconds grants a 15% damage buff, 20% defense buff, and prevents from being knocked for 10 seconds.

Tidal Shock: [Cala-Mariner](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Cala-Mariner)- Landing a successful Critical Attack will temporarily put you in a state where you dodge all mantras, as well as dazing your opponent.

- Your Critical attacks will apply Daze on hit.
- For the next 4s after landing your Critical attack, if you were to be hit by any Mantra, you'll automatically dodge the Mantra, voiding its damage. The dodge vfx is similar to [Nautilodaunt's](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Nautilodaunt) Vent vfx, but it displaces you physically.
- 13 second cooldown.

Umami: [Chef's Toque](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Chef%27s_Toque)- Feast on foes with mighty cutlery. Chef Weapons now steal opponents hunger.

- Has a 35% chance to proc with a 3 cooldown per proc attempt, going on cooldown even if it fails to proc.
- Hunger and thirst steal formula is as follows: 1.95 * ln(D) + 1.075, where D = scaled damage dealt. A calculator is listed [here](https://www.desmos.com/calculator/yktgme7xnc).
- The hunger and thirst steal are NOT affected by damage modifiers or resistances, rather exclusively being based on scaled damage dealt.
- Acts similarly to and stacks additively with the Gluttony enchant.
- Procs on: [The Long Tong of The Law](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/The_Long_Tong_of_The_Law), [The Flippers of Fate](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/The_Flippers_of_Fate), [The Pastry Paster](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/The_Pastry_Paster), and [Fondant Splitter](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Fondant_Splitter).

Unbreakable: [Darksteel Plate](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Darksteel_Plate)& [Etrean Guard](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Etrean_Guard)- You have reduced stun duration when block broken.

- Reduces the normal stun duration from 1.05 seconds to 0.7 seconds.

Vigil's Grace: [Vigil Sentinel](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vigil_Sentinel)- Whenever you gain a speed boost, increase your swingspeed by 0.02.

- Stacks additively with Action Surge.
- Both this and Action Surge are applied before [Lightning Cloak's](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Lightning_Cloak) swing speed multiplier.

Volt Draft: [Specialist Helmet](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Specialist_Helmet)- Using Inhale will now also make your Basic Attacks apply a storm strike. [20 second CD]

- This has a proc window of 5 seconds after using Inhale.
- Storm Strikes from this Talent deal 0.125 * (Thundercall + Galebreathe investment) Thundercall damage, capping at 25 damage with 100 points invested in both Attunements.
- These Storm Strikes proc Grounding Bolt.

Volt Reflex: [Authority Voltspark Mask](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Authority_Voltspark_Mask)- Your dodge window is increased with the power of lightning, but also increase the cooldown of your dodges.

- Increases the dodge window based on your [Thundercall](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Thundercall) investment.
- Increases your dodge cooldown for both dodge types to 2.4 seconds regardless of your Thundercall investment.

Way of the Wind: [Gale Enhanced Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Gale_Enhanced_Earrings)& [Gale Master's Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Gale_Master%27s_Earrings)- Being in the air briefly increases the speed of your mantra casts. This effect only lasts while you're in the air.

- On proc, a wind of gale will erupt from your legs, signifying that the effect is active.

Withering Soul: [Darkened Bastion](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Darkened_Bastion)- Your Basic Attacks proc 12.5% of their damage as Wither. Wielding the [Deepspindle](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Deepspindle) or [Umbrite Witherblade](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Umbrite_Witherblade) will double the amount to 25%.

- The Wither applied is based off your weapon's scaled damage.

Whistleguard: [Whistling Periapt](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Whistling_Periapt)- The favor of the winds is yours: landing three hits (five for light weapons) without taking damage against your opponent envelops you in a shield of wind.

- The wind shield grants one (1) autoparry frame and has a cooldown of 10 seconds.
- The wind shield lasts a few seconds or until you get hit (in which case, it will automatically parry the attack), cast any mantra, feint any attack, or use your critical attack.
- Some mantras, like [Mecha Gatling](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Mecha_Gatling), can build up stacks for this.
- If you have the wind shield active and a Talent that allows you to parry unparryable monster attacks, such as Force Your Way or Diver's Resilience, you will autoparry them.

Wind Dancer: [Windrunner Robes](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Windrunner_Robes)- Roll cancelling twice in quick succession will grant you a speed boost. One of your rolls must dodge a hit.

- Grants a 10% speed boost for 8 seconds.
- 10 second cooldown.

Winter's Protection [Winter Corps Parka](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Winter_Corps_Parka)- Your tightly-bound winter gear negates elemental damage buffs from weather on damage against you. Also seems to provide some resistance to the Gale.

Negates damage buffs from weather:

- [Frostdraw](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Frostdraw) will no longer apply its 5% damage buff during snow.
- [Thundercall](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Thundercall) will no longer gain its 5% damage buff during rain. Stratoshock will also no longer apply its additional 5% damage buff.

- Woodland Terrain: [Woodland Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Woodland_Boots)- You move slightly faster on grass.

Wormwarder: [Wormwarder Lantern](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Wormwarder_Lantern)- Your lantern generates a shroud of static electricity around you that the flesh-burrowing parasites of the Eternal Gale detest. It's not exactly the lightest thing to lug around, though.

- Makes you entirely immune to the [Second Layer's](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Second_Layer) Parasites, but at a cost of granting you a mobility debuff.

## Set Talents

Certain Talents are given when you equip a full equipment set, though not all equipment sets have unique Set Talents. These Talents are exclusive to that equipment set and are unobtainable by any other means. Set Talents are listed in alphabetical order:

Bloodless Overdrive: [Necrotic Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)/ [Bloodcurdle Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)- Gain bonus [temp health](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Temporary_Health) everytime you land a mantra with a [Bloodless Gem](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Bloodless_Gem).

- Grants 50% of the Bloodless Gem healing as Temporary Health.
- Necrotic Set: [Necrotic Cowl](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Necrotic_Cowl), [Necrotic Mask](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Necrotic_Mask)
- Bloodcurdle Set: [Bloodcurdle Cowl](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Bloodcurdle_Cowl), [Bloodcurdle Mask](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Bloodcurdle_Mask)

Captain's Call: [Ossified Black Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)/ [Blackalloy Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)- Call up two [Blacksteel Pirates](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Blacksteel_Pirate) to aid you in battle.

- On use, this summons two allied [Blacksteel Pirates](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Blacksteel_Pirate) to assist you. 5 minute cooldown.
- Ossified Black Set: [Ossified Black Helm](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ossified_Black_Helm), [Ossified Black Pauldrons](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ossified_Black_Pauldrons)
- Blackalloy Set: [Blackalloy Helm](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Blackalloy_Helm), [Blackalloy Pauldrons](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Blackalloy_Pauldrons)

Critical Heal: [Regenerative Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)/ [Trueblood Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)- Every time you heal, you have a chance to proc a Critical Heal, healing you 30 health. [5 second CD]

- Regenerative Set: [Regenerative Pendant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Regenerative_Pendant), [Regenerative Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Regenerative_Earrings)
- Trueblood Set: [Trueblood Pendant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Trueblood_Pendant), [Trueblood Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Trueblood_Earrings)

Enforcer's Pull: [Hardened Enforcer Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)/ [Reinforced Enforcer Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)- Your [Enforcer's Pull](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Enforcer_Pull) is improved. Land a flourish to make it even stronger.

- Hardened Enforcer Set: [Hardened Enforcer Plate](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Hardened_Enforcer_Plate), [Hardened Enforcer Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Hardened_Enforcer_Boots)
- Reinforced Enforcer Set: [Reinforced Enforcer Plate](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Reinforced_Enforcer_Plate), [Reinforced Enforcer Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Reinforced_Enforcer_Boots)

Etherguard: [Blustone Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)- Warped Blue Gems now provide a bonus effect when landed using a mantra, Etherguard. This effect gives extra resistances to mantras based on how much ether they cost to cast + gain elemental protection against PvE.

- When landing a Mantra with a [Warped Blue Gem](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Warped_Blue_Gem) equipped, gain the [Etherguard](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Etherguard) status effect, granting you damage reduction to Mantras. This damage reduction scales on the Ether cost of the Mantra you got hit by.
- In PvE, Etherguard additionally grants elemental damage reduction.
- Bluestone Set: [Bluestone Pauldrons](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Bluestone_Pauldrons), [Bluestone Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Bluestone_Boots)

Lasting Trauma: [Reinforced War Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)- Anytime you land a Wrath Gem, your opponent becomes fearful, being unable to use their critical for a few seconds after you land it.

- This applies the [Fearful](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Fearful) status effect for 19 seconds.
- Wrath gems have a 10 second cooldown, meaning you can potentially disable your opponent's ability to use their critical indefinitely.
- Reinforced War Set: [Reinforced War Helmet](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Reinforced_War_Helmet), [Reinforced War Plate](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Reinforced_War_Plate), [Reinforced War Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Reinforced_War_Boots)

Offensive Recovery: [Ascended Outlaw Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)- If all your mantras have [Might Gems](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Might_Gem) equipped, lose posture any time you hit opponents block with a mantra.

- If all of your Mantras have a [Might Gem](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Might_Gem) equipped, hitting your opponent's block with Mantras will restore your posture.
- This functions somewhat similarly to Arcwarder Mantras while in Arc suit.
- Ascended Outlaw Set: [Ascended Outlaw Hat](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ascended_Outlaw_Hat), [Ascended Outlaw Robes](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ascended_Outlaw_Robes), [Ascended Outlaw Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ascended_Outlaw_Boots)

Shock Trooper Specialist: [Specialist Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)- Empower the mantras taught to you by your Legion Specialist Captain. You also now conjure a storm strike on your opponent by landing Gale and Thundercall mantras.

- To proc Shock Trooper Specialist, you must meet the a damage threshold of 600 damage dealt with Thunder and Gale Mantras. Each point in Galebreathe or Thundercall reduces this threshold by 1, capping at 400 damage dealt for a Storm Strike with 100 Gale and Thunder.
- The damage threshold does not consider damage multipliers, but it does consider resistances, making it harder to proc the higher Wind/Lightning res your opponent has.
- The Storm Strikes deal 40 Thundercall damage.
- Shock Trooper Specialist has no cooldown, proccing on almost every hit in PvE due to the PvE damage multipliers. However, this only has 1.08x PvE scaling.
- Additionally, this turns your [Electro Carve](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Electro_Carve), [Grand Javelin](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Grand_Javelin), and [Thunder Kick](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Thunder_Kick) green, and your [Astral Wind](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Astral_Wind), [Champion's Whirlthrow](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Champion%27s_Whirlthrow), and [Gale Lunge](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Gale_Lunge) yellow. These Mantras will gain a 15% damage buff to their base damage (not a damage modifier) as well.
- The storm strikes proc Grounding Bolt.
- Specialist Set: [Specialist Helmet](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Specialist_Helmet), [Specialist Plate](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Specialist_Plate), [Specialist Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Specialist_Boots)

Sorcerer's Supply: [Ether Refined Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)/ [Sorcerer Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)- Increase the amount of [Tempo](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Tempo) you have by 30.

- Ether Refined Set: [Ether Refined Brim](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ether_Refined_Brim), [Ether Refined Mantle](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ether_Refined_Mantle)
- Sorcerer Set: [Sorcerer's Brim](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Sorcerer%27s_Brim), [Sorcerer's Mantle](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Sorcerer%27s_Mantle)

## Blacklisted Talents

Certain Talents cannot be obtained through Roll 2, as they come with significant drawbacks, are mutually exclusive with other Talents, or could otherwise break certain builds.

- Any Rare Talent unless it has been [frozen](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Aces).
- Any attunement path's starting Talent (e.g. Wraith Path: Twisted Puppets, Eruption Path: Lava Serpent, or Glass Path: Crystallization)
- Dancing Steps - Fire mantras now move you forward slightly.
- Nullifying Clarity - Deal more damage to enemies with status effects on them with your Basic Attacks but remove the effect on hit.
- Agitating Spark - If you hit an opponent that you've lit on fire, it spreads to yourself and others around you.

## Vow of Iron Exclusive Talents

Many Talents are exclusive to the [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) gamemode. These exclusive Talents will be listed here by category, in alphabetical order. Keep in mind that many of the non-Vow of Iron exclusive Talents are still obtainable in this gamemode, but they will not be listed in this section.

### Berserker

Residual Fury [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - In Berserk State Landing your critical grants +50% PEN on Basic Attacks for 8s.

- Prerequisites: [Berserker](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Berserker) Memento, purchased from the Power 10 shop

Righteous Rage [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - In Berserk State You deal increased damage the lower your health is.

- [Temporary Health](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Temporary_Health) does not count for this effect.
- Prerequisites: [Berserker](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Berserker) Memento, defeat [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity)

Rip and Tear [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Regain health upon defeating an enemy.

- Prerequisites: [Berserker](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Berserker) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard (Temple of Mur)](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur))

Ruinous Recovery [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Enemies recover 15% less Posture when parrying. Guardbreaking an enemy by parrying them deals massive damage, and can be done against even the strongest of foes.

- Prerequisites: [Berserker](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Berserker) Memento, purchased from the starting shop

Until It Is Done [Memento Talent] - Your [Berserk State](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Berserk_State) lasts until you willingly end it.

- Your Berserk now has an infinite duration, only turning off when you use the Talent tool again.
- Prerequisite: [Berserker](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Berserker) Memento

### Breaker

Breakthrough [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Posture damage on light attacks are increased by 10%.

- Increases the posture damage of M1s by 10%.
