- Prerequisites: [Breaker](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Breaker) Memento, purchased from the Power 5 shop

Calamity Punch [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Every successful [Strong Left](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Strong_Left) builds up stacks to perform a larger scale punch.

Dormant Strength [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - At max Calamity Punch stacks increased posture damage & physical damage.

- Increases your [Strong Left](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Strong_Left)'s damage and posture damage if you are at max Calamity Punch stacks.
- Prerequisites: [Breaker](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Breaker) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard (Temple of Mur)](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur))

Relentless Barrage [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Every swing from [Rapid Punches](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rapid_Punches) deals more posture than the last. At max punch stacks enables hyperarmor during the barrage.

- Your Rapid Punches posture damage per hit scales on the amount of hits landed/blocked.
- At max Calamity Punch stacks, gain hyperarmor during Rapid Punches.
- Prerequisites: [Breaker](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Breaker) Memento, purchased from the Power 15 shop

Shattering Left [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your [Strong Left](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Strong_Left) and [Wind-up](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Wind-Up) punches will always guard break mobs.

- Turns your Strong Left and Wind-Up enhanced attacks into guaranteed guardbreaks against blocking NPCs.
- Prerequisites: [Breaker](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Breaker) Memento, defeat [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King)

Threefold Impact [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Every stack built with [Strong Left](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Strong_Left) provides a buff. 1st successful attack will reduces cooldown, 2nd ignores armor, and 3rd increases damage & guardbreaks.

Grants buffs to your Strong Left based on how many Calamity Punch stacks you have.

- With one stack, your Strong Left cooldown will be reduced by ?s.
- With two stacks, your Strong Left will ignore your opponent's armor, on top of the first stack bonus.
- With three stacks, your Strong Left will deal additional damage and guardbeak, on top of the first and second stack bonuses.

### Chaotic Moon

Blessing of the Moonseye [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - The Moonseye is not one eye, but many. All of them watch you. All of them know of your sins.

Defeating the [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard (Temple of Mur)](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur)), [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King), and [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity) while having this Talent will unlock the [Prince of the Moon](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Prince_of_the_Moon) Memento.

- Equipment such as the [Deranged Treasure](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Deranged_Treasure), [Oni Mask](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Oni_Mask), and [Heretic's Moonseye Gauntlets](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Heretic%27s_Moonseye_Gauntlets)(unexhaustive) can greatly assist you in this task, as they provide Talents and effects that greatly enhance your character at low Sanity, or allow you to perform actions at the cost of a percentage of your Sanity (free at 0 Sanity).
- Additionally, if you time your shivers, using [Levit](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Levit)'s Retrograde may allow you to shrug off any unavoidable damage you would normally take by shivering and scratching yourself.

### Ether Conduit

Ether Blade [Common Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Draw your foes ether into your weapon when you parry ether-based attacks. Gain Ether when you parry mantras.

- Prerequisites: [Strange Merchant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Strange_Merchant) Memento, purchased from the starting shop

### Drifter

Blinkstep [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your regular dash is enhanced into a Blinkstep above 90% ether but now has an ether cost.

- Replaces your dash with a longer and faster dash that costs some ether.
- Cannot be used if your Ether is below 90%. Having the True Drifter Talent will remove this requirement, assuming you have stacks of Cutting Pace.
- Prerequisites: [Drifter](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Drifter) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur))

Cutting Pace [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Dodging attacks will now grant 'Cutting Pace' stacks. Mantras and basic attacks consume a stack to shorten wind-up and increase damage, procs after dash.

- Buffed attacks deal 15% more damage, have a slightly shorter windup, and are recolored to a purple.
- Prerequisites: [Drifter](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Drifter) Memento, defeat [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King)

Drift [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - You're no longer slow enough to have to rely on parries. Dodge instead.

- Replaces your parry with a fast dodge that can dodge unparryable attacks, at the cost of no parry stun. You still need to be hit by something for the dodge to occur.
- Prerequisite: [Drifter](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Drifter) Memento

Drifting Cloud [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Cutting Pace now unsheathes the full potential of your [Purple Cloud](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Purple_Cloud).

- Upon proccing Cutting Pace, unsheathe your Purple Cloud.
- This changes Purple Cloud's stats, animations, and damage type to Slash. Additionally Purple Cloud gains a unique swing trail.
- The unsheathed Purple Cloud stays until you leave the game.
- Prerequisites: [Drifter](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Drifter) Memento, defeat [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King)

Faster Blade [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - A successful "Drift" now grants a speed boost

- Procs when "parrying" an attack.
- Prerequisites: [Drifter](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Drifter) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur))

Lackluster Guard [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - You aren't used to having to block, resulting in weaker posture.

- Reduces your maximum posture.
- Prerequisite: [Drifter](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Drifter) Memento

True Drifter [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - You are a true drifter. Gain double the 'Cutting Pace' stacks and consume stacks to Blinkstep below 90% ether.

- Gain double the stacks whenever you proc Cutting Pace. Additionally, you can now Blinkstep when below 90% Ether by consuming a Cutting Pace stack.
- Prerequisites: [Drifter](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Drifter) Memento, defeat [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity)

Twin Drift [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Hitting an enemy in the back after a roll cancel will bring upon twins who will follow up your assault.

- Similarly to Spine Cutter , performing a basic attack after a roll cancel will perform a second slash, then two light purple clones of yourself will attack the target.
- Prerequisites: [Drifter](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Drifter) Memento, defeat [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity)

### Ether Erudite

Barrage Mastery [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - For every Ether Erudite stack you have, gain one extra orb whenever you fire your Ether Barrage.

- Prerequisites: [Ether Erudite](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ether_Erudite) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur))

Ether Amplification [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - For every Ether Erudite Stack you have, increase the size and damage of your [Pressure Blast](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Pressure_Blast).

- Turns Pressure Blast light blue.
- Prerequisites: [Ether Erudite](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ether_Erudite) Memento, defeat [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King)

Ether Blitz [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - For every Ether Erudite Stack you have, reduce the windup of your enhanced [Master's Flourish](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Master%27s_Flourish). This consumes your Ether Erudite stacks on use.

- Turns Master's Flourish light blue.
- Prerequisites: [Ether Erudite](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ether_Erudite) Memento, defeat [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King)

Ether Burst [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Landing [Rapid Slashes](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rapid_Slashes) on your opponent gives them an overflow of Ether, causing them to take bursts of damage based on how many Ether Erudite stacks you had.

- Consumes your Ether Erudite Stacks.
- Turns your Rapid Slashes light blue.
- Prerequisites: [Ether Erudite](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ether_Erudite) Memento, defeat [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity)

Ether Erudite [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - The more total Ether you have, the more damage you deal. Gain Ether Erudite Stacks to improve your combat abilities. The max amount you can hold is 6.

- Prerequisite: [Ether Erudite](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ether_Erudite) Memento

Ether Quell [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Landing a Critical Attack or Guardbreaking an opponent gives you 1 Ether Erudite Stack.

- Prerequisites: [Ether Erudite](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ether_Erudite) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur))

Ether Reflux [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Successfully countering an opponent with [Prediction](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Prediction) gives you 2 Ether Erudite stacks.

- Prerequisites: [Ether Erudite](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ether_Erudite) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur))

### Flame Inheritor

Flames of the Past [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your flamecharm mantras now call forth firey homing spirits.

- Your Flamecharm Mantras now spawn Twisted Puppets' Puppets.
- Prerequisites: [Flame Worshipper](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Flame_Worshipper) Memento, defeat [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity)

### Flame Warden

Pleeksty's Wrath [Common Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - After dealing 500 damage with Flame Mantras, call forth a hail of firey swords. (+4 Sanity)

- Spawns swords from the sky, similarly to [First Light](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/First_Light)'s critical attack.
- Prerequisites: [Flame Worshipper](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Flame_Worshipper) Memento, defeat [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King)

### Flashing Thunder

Electrical Accumulation [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - While having 4 or more orbs, automatically gain 4 charges of Static Blade whilst you have 4 orbs.

- Prerequisites: [Flashing Thunder](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Flashing_Thunder) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard (Temple of Mur)](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur))

Gathering Storm [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your damage is stored in a lightning orb. Sheathing your weapon will shoot the lightning orbs at the opponent. The orbs will do more damage based on how many orbs are being stored up.

- Weapon damage is stored in lightning orbs above your character's head. Sheathing your weapon will consume all of the orbs and deal the stored damage all at once.
- New orbs will be created once you reach certain thresholds of stored damage. You cannot have more than 8 orbs at once, but your stored damage will still be accumulated past this point.
- This is essentially the [Poser's Ring](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Poser%27s_Ring) effect but with a visual aspect.
- Prerequisite: [Flashing Thunder](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Flashing_Thunder) Memento

Heir of Lightning [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - At max orbs, [Lightning Cloak](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Lightning_Cloak) is enhanced.

- Triples the base duration of [Lightning Cloak](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Lightning_Cloak).
- Prerequisites: [Flashing Thunder](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Flashing_Thunder) Memento, defeat [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity)

Orb Discharge [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - If an extra orb is available, you can consume it to vent if you don't have enough tempo.

- Prerequisites: [Flashing Thunder](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Flashing_Thunder) Memento, defeat [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King)

### Heliodar

Command Phoenix [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Command your [Phoenix](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Solborn) to scout, and dive into enemies.

- Gives a Talent tool that allows you to command your Phoenix to attack an enemy.
- Using the tool at low Heartfire will summon a smaller version of the Phoenix, which can only be used as a [campfire](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Campfire).
- Summoning an adult Phoenix, then commanding it to take air will allow you to select a spot where it should Divebomb on the next usage of the Talent.
- Prerequisite: [Solborn](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Solborn) Memento

Heliodar Wings [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Take flight [H].

- Gives a Talent tool that allows you to fly. This flight will passively drain your Heartfire Gauge. Using the wings with an empty Heartfire Gauge will set you on fire.
- Attempting to use this during the [Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rat_King) fight will cause him to use the Sparkle Blitz move.
- While flying, hold Space to ascend, CTRL to descend, and Q to speed up.
- Prerequisites: [Solborn](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Solborn) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard (Temple of Mur)](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur))

### Immolator

Ascended Phoenix Flames [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Once per fight, when an attack would down you immediately revive with 30% HP.

- Prerequisites: [Flame Worshipper](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Flame_Worshipper) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard (Temple of Mur)](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur)) OR [Solborn](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Solborn) Memento, defeat [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity)

### Inheritor of the Gale

Prince of the Wind [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - You are the one who the wind bends around. Your galebreathe is enhanced.

- Prerequisite: [Inheritor of the Gale](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Inheritor_of_the_Gale) Memento

### Innate

Talents granted from your [Aspect](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Aspect). You will additionally gain the default Aspect Talents from your respective Aspect.

Hardened Shell [Race Talent, [Vesperian](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vesperian), [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your Natural Armor grows sturdier, being able to take more hits before breaking.

- Gives a flat amount of armor durability to your Natural Armor.

Steady Mind [Race Talent, [Ganymede](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ganymede), [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Despite the finality of your mind and body succumbing to the effects of the depths, you manage to ward it off for a little longer.

- You can spend more time in [The Depths](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/The_Depths_(Vow_of_Iron)) before gaining [Afflictions](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Afflictions).

Glide Mastery [Race Talent, [Tiran](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Tiran), [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Experience using the glider you crafted results in you being able to glide faster in the air. You've also trained to quickly pull it out in dire situations, resulting in you no longer taking fall damage.

- Increases glide speed and grants full fall damage immunity.

Acute Hearing [Race Talent, [Kiron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Kiron), [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - After years of using Echolocation, you've trained your ears to sense danger from even further away. You are also able to sense when someone completes a job.

- Plays a sound effect whenever Echolocation procs.

- From The Ashes [Race Talent, [Heliodar](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Heliodar), [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Using an [Etris Flask](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Etris_Flask) when at 30% health or below now grants you a damage buff and resistance buff for 30 seconds.

- Lone Wolf [Race Talent, [Canor](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Canor), [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Despite the loyalty of your allies, you recognize that you sometimes need to hunt alone. Deal more damage to targets who are only combat tagged by you.

Scales of The Edenkite [Race Talent, [Drakkard](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Drakkard), [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - In order to resolve conflict in Lumen, you need to be able to handle any situation thrown at you. Activate your Scales to reduce all damage taken by 80% and de-aggro enemies briefly.

- Gives a Talent Tool.
- The buff lasts 5 seconds.
- 3 minute cooldown.
- The deaggro effect is extremely ineffective, as enemies are very likely to reaggro as soon as the effect ends. Additionally, it does not work against bosses or in Depths trials.
- The Talent tool's description is very outdated, listing incorrect information.

Scholar's Mark [Race Talent, [Capra](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Capra), [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - You now are able to inflict yourself with a Mark. The duration of this scales with how many people you inflict with the effect as well.

- Capra Marks will now buff yourself.
- The duration of self-applied Capra Marks scale with how many people you've buffed.

- Largent [Race Talent, [Adret](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Adret), [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your diplomatic skills extend into your ability to barter. Gain 300 extra notes per chest when turning in a sack.

- Tailor Made [Race Talent, [Etrean](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Etrean), [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - These were specifically made for your kind. [Etris Flasks](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Etris_Flask) now heal you 45% more than usual.

- Mothwing Defense [Race Talent, [Chrysid](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Chrysid), [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Further refining how sharp your antennae, you can now immediately recognize a sneak attack and brace yourself for it. Reduce all backhit damage by 50%.

- Navae's Technique [Race Talent, [Gremor](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Gremor), [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - A passed down technique from generations ago to refine your Ether control. Reduce how much ether it takes to cast mantras by 30%.

- Innate Agility [Race Talent, [Felinor](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Felinor), [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - You hinge on your instincts to keep you alive, even in battle. Slightly increase your dodge frames.

Retrograde [Race Talent, [Levit](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Levit), [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your existence is... questionable. No one understands where you have come from. Your ability reflects this, activate to start your Retrograde timer. Activating the ability again will reverse your actions, with the cooldown scaling with the time.

- Grants a Talent tool. On use, your current position will be anchored, and you can perform any action. If Retrograde is cast again or if 5 seconds pass, your actions will be done in reverse, returning you to your original location.
- This will heal yourself to the amount of health you had when you initially used the ability.

- Depth's Denizen [Race Talent, [Celtor](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Celtor), [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - The city you once called home is now your only way out of the depths, perhaps you can use this to your advantage. Gain extra leeway when attempting to escape the depths.

- Weapons Training [Race Talent, [Khan](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Khan), [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Training with different weapons taught you how to wield all of them more proficiently. Gain slightly extra weapon scaling on all weapons you use.

### Ironsinger

Alloy of the Heavens [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Posture attack from mobs reduced by 50%

- Reduces posture damage taken from mobs by 50%.
- Prerequisites: [Iron Vessel](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Iron_Vessel) Memento, purchased from the Power 10 shop

### Merchant

Deal Maker [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your rapport with merchants allows you to attach a flat fee of 20 notes to all your sales.

- Increases the sell price of all items by 20 notes.
- This is applied after Snake Oil.
- Prerequisites: [Strange Merchant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Strange_Merchant) Memento, purchased from the starting shop

Know When To Run [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your magic coffin has a higher chance to draw depths loot, but the price is increased.

- Increases the price of your [Magic Coffin](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Strange_Merchant) by 50%.
- Adds the entire [Depths](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/The_Depths_(Vow_of_Iron)) lootpool to the Coffin.
- Prerequisites: [Strange Merchant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Strange_Merchant) Memento, defeat [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King)

Stack the Deck [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Charmed enemies will grant you even more damage multipliers on your Taunt counter.

- Prerequisites: [Strange Merchant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Strange_Merchant) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard (Temple of Mur)](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur))

### Prince of the Moon

Soul Veil [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - [Soulthorn](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Soulthorn) now directly siphons the song of your enemies. Accumulating stacks and increasing your power. Perfect plays are encouraged.

- Landing basic attacks with the Soulthorn grants a stacking 7.5% damage buff. This caps at +30% damage.
- If you get hit while having stacks of Soul Veil, your attacker will steal your stacks for themselves. You can steal your stacks back by hitting them.
- Prerequisite: [Prince of the Moon](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Prince_of_the_Moon) Memento

### Rogue Assassin

Ambush [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - When coming out of [Tacet](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Tacet), gain a 30% damage boost for 5 seconds.

- Prerequisites: [Rogue Assassin](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rogue_Assassin) Memento, defeat [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King)

Avaricious Blade [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Every strike landed lines your pockets.

- Dealing damage by any means gives 1.
- Backhits give 50 instead.
- These effects also proc on self damage.
- Prerequisite: [Rogue Assassin](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rogue_Assassin) Memento

Double Jump [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Take the Wind Step and refine it into your own technique, reducing the cooldown and improving its efficiency.

- Reduces Wind Step's cooldown to 5 seconds and reduces the Ether cost.
- Because this Memento does not have Wind Step innately, you will have to gain it through the [Gale Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Gale_Boots).
- Prerequisites: [Rogue Assassin](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rogue_Assassin) Memento, defeat [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King)

Fatal Backstab [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Weapon backstabs deal an extra 5 true damage on your opponent (20 extra damage if done to PvE).

- Prerequisites: [Rogue Assassin](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rogue_Assassin) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard (Temple of Mur)](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur))

Lethal Depressant [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Assassinating an opponent decreases the damage they deal by 25% for the next 30 seconds.

- The description of this Talent is identical to Weakening Venom. It is unclear whether this is intended.
- Prerequisites: [Rogue Assassin](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rogue_Assassin) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard (Temple of Mur)](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur))

Rogue Assassin [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your assassinations now deal an extra 50 true damage (200 in PvE).

- Prerequisite: [Rogue Assassin](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rogue_Assassin) Memento

Terrifying Paralysis [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your [Hidden Blade](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Hidden_Blade) disables opponents' ability to roll for 3 seconds.

- Prerequisites: [Rogue Assassin](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rogue_Assassin) Memento, defeat [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity)

Total Silence [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Massively reduce the sound of your footsteps and rolls.

- Prerequisites: [Rogue Assassin](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rogue_Assassin) Memento, purchased from the Power 20 shop

True Skulk [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Massively reduce the sounds of your mantra casts.

- Prerequisites: [Rogue Assassin](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rogue_Assassin) Memento, defeat [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity)

Weakening Venom [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Assassinating an opponent decreases the damage they deal by 25% for the next 30 seconds.

- Prerequisite: [Rogue Assassin](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rogue_Assassin) Memento

### Saint Jay

Frostride [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your footsteps echo the legend of Saint Jay, freezing the sea beneath.

- You can now walk on water by creating ice below your feet. This has no cooldown and does not cost Ether.
- Prerequisites: [Saint Jay](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Saint_Jay) Memento, purchased from the Power 10 shop

Jay's Judgement Gavel [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Breaking a frozen enemy free with your Critical saps their lifeforce.

- Landing your critical on a frozen or bottom frozen enemy will deal extra damage and heal you.
- Prerequisite: [Saint Jay](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Saint_Jay) Memento

Rebuke in Stasis [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Bottom freeze enemies nearby when countering with [Punishment](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Punishment), freezes mobs affected when re-casted.

- Your Punishment will apply bottom freeze to mobs if you land the counter hit.
- Prerequisites: [Saint Jay](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Saint_Jay) Memento, purchased from the starting shop

### Saintsworn

Verses of Harmony [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Each elemental stance grants access to select talents from the corresponding attunement.

Verses of Harmony grants the following Talents;

- Flamecharm: Base - Dancing Steps, Immolation | Power 5 - Agitating Spark
- Frostdraw: Base - Condensation Drip, Saint Jay | Power 5 - Frozen Legs | Power 10 - Chilling Flourish, Fragile Freeze
- Galebreathe: Base - Pressure Break, Wind Step | Power 5 - Air Pressure | Power 10 - Cyclone Blade, Inhale | Power 15 - A World Without Song
- Shadowcast: Base - Dark Rift | Power 5 - Lasting Sorrows | Power 10 - Dark God, Dark Hours | Power 15 Shadow Overflow | Power 20 - Singularity
- Thundercall: Base - Static Fakeout, Stratoshock | Power 5 - Resolve Crusher | Power 10 - Static Blade | Power 15 - Gathering Electricty

### Sightless

Focused Perception [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Massively increase the range of your [Rhythm](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rhythm) if you use it while [Tranquil Circle](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Tranquil_Circle) is active.

- The Sightless Memento does not unlock Tranquil Circle at any point, causing this Talent to do nothing.
- Prerequisites: [Sightless](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Sightless) Memento, defeat [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King)

Purge Shot [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Landing an [arc beam](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Sightless#Arc_Beam) while your opponent has anti heal stacks purges the stacks, with it dealing 5 true damage for every stack they had.

- Landing Arc Beam removes all stacks of anti-heal from your opponent, dealing 5 true damage for every stack they had.
- Prerequisites: [Sightless](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Sightless) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard (Temple of Mur)](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur))

Scornful Scowl [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your [Glare](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Glare) stuns enemies for much longer.

- Prerequisites: [Sightless](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Sightless) Memento, defeat [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity)

Unmended Eye [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - All mantras you land now apply anti heal.

- Prerequisites: [Sightless](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Sightless) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard (Temple of Mur)](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur))

### Solborn

Ignition Rite [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Landing fire attacks with the phoenix summoned regenerates Heartfire.

- Prerequisites: [Solborn](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Solborn) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard (Temple of Mur)](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur))

Heartfire Renewal [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Regenerate Heartfire whenever you are burning. Upon revival with Phoenix Flames, restore half of your Heartfire gauge.

- Prerequisites: [Solborn](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Solborn) Memento, defeat [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King)

Pyreborne: [Memento Talent, Equipment Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your Flamecharm mantras have all stats amplified

- Increases the base damage of all Flamecharm Mantras by 30%
- Obtained from: [Emberseal Pendant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Emberseal_Pendant)

Solborn [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Harness your Heartfire to command the Phoenix bound to your soul.

- Spawns a small orange, stationary, Phoenix near you. The size of the Phoenix will increase based on the user's level.
- This Phoenix can be used like a campfire to rest and heal yourself.
- Utilized in many other Solborn Talents.
- Prerequisite: [Solborn](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Solborn) Memento

### Spear Dancer

Dancer's Beat [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your [Vent](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vent) is replaced with Dancer's Beat. You increase the speed, damage, and stun of your next Basic Attack. However, should your attack fail to land, you'll be stunned and your [Tempo](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Tempo) will be greatly reduced.

- Your Vent can no longer be used defensively, but does not cost tempo to use. You need to have at least 20 Tempo to use Dancer's Beat.
- Using your Vent will put you in a stance for a brief moment. Your next M1 used within 1 second of using Dancer's Beat will deal higher damage, more stun, and have a higher swing speed.
- If you are hit before you use your next attack, wait out the 1 second proc window, or if your next attack does not land (including if it was blocked, parried, or dodged), you will lose 60 Tempo and will be stunned for 0.5 seconds. During this stun you will not be able to perform any actions, but this stun will not cancel any of your currently active attacks.
- The Dancer's Beat Vent has a 2.5 second cooldown compared to the default 10 seconds.
- Dancer's Beat can still be used to escape stun locking attacks like [Judgement](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Judgement) and [Zi'eer, the Fourth Prophet](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Zi%27eer,_the_Fourth_Prophet)'s attacks.
- Prerequisite: [Spear Dancer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Spear_Dancer) Memento

Dancer's Geas [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - You deal +30% damage when striking with the tip of your weapon, but deal 15% less damage when striking below half of its range.

- When you land an attack from the tip of your weapon, an icon of a pair of hands clapping will appear, signifying that you've activated the buff.
- When you land an attack from half or less than half of your weapon's range, an icon of a thumbs down will appear, signifying that you were debuffed.
- Also procs on weapon criticals.
- Because of how monster hitboxes work, you will almost always benefit from the positive effect of this Talent while fighting non-humanoids, regardless of how close to them are are.
- Prerequisite: [Spear Dancer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Spear_Dancer) Memento

Dancer's Impale [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Hitting an enemy after a perfect dodge makes your next attack deal 30% bleed damage. If that attack would already bleed, it adds +10% chip damage instead. (+4 Passive Agility)

- Essentially, this is Lancer's Impale without being weapon type locked.
- Prerequisites: [Spear Dancer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Spear_Dancer) Memento, purchased from the starting shop

Dancer's Sting [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Striking with the tip of your weapon applies +25% PEN and +25% Chip Damage.

- Prerequisites: [Spear Dancer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Spear_Dancer) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard (Temple of Mur)](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur))

Ga Buidhe [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Backstabs now count as the tip of your weapon.

- Attacking your opponent's back now procs all Talents and effects that would normally proc when attacking with the tip of your weapon.
- Prerequisites: [Spear Dancer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Spear_Dancer) Memento, defeat [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King)

Ga Dearg [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Striking a bleeding enemy with the tip of your weapon extends the bleed.

- Hitting a bleeding enemy with the tip of your weapon will reapply the bleed.
- Prerequisites: [Spear Dancer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Spear_Dancer) Memento, purchased from the starting shop

Kick the Beat [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Dancer's Beat now works with Critical Attacks.

- Prerequisites: [Spear Dancer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Spear_Dancer) Memento, purchased from the Power 15 shop

Not My Tempo [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Dancer's Beat grants Hyperarmor.

- Prerequisites: [Spear Dancer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Spear_Dancer) Memento, defeat [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity)

Two Left Feet [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Posture breaking an opponent grants you +50% PEN for 3 seconds.

- 14 second cooldown.
- Essentially, this is Defensive Sweep without being weapon type locked.
- Prerequisites: [Spear Dancer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Spear_Dancer) Memento, purchased from the Power 10 shop

### Steampunk Soldier

Explosive Ignition [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Ignite nearby steam, causing a large explosion.

- Grants a Talent tool, that when used while standing inside of a steam cloud causes a very large explosion after a delay, dealing high damage and consuming the Steam cloud.
- Prerequisites: [Steampunk Soldier](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Steampunk_Soldier) Memento, defeat [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity)

Mist Mobility [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Anytime you proc Boiling Point, increase the speed at which you reload your [Vapormaw Carbine](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vapormaw_Carbine) and increase your Critical Attack's fire rate.

- Despite what the description states, this actually procs when you activate Flashboil, and not Boiling Point.
- Prerequisites: [Steampunk Soldier](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Steampunk_Soldier) Memento, defeat [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King)

Steampunk Soldier [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Increase the size of your steam which you activate Flashboil. Flashboil's cooldown is also reduced.

- Prerequisite: [Steampunk Soldier](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Steampunk_Soldier) Memento

### Steelvessel

Alloy Siphon [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Iron Pull siphons armor from your foes, restoring your posture.

- Prerequisites: [Iron Vessel](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Iron_Vessel) Memento, defeat [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity)

Steelvessel [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Landed attacks deal posture damage but when guard broken take massive damage. Posture gain on parry is reduced.

Take posture damage instead of health damage. You can be guardbroken by being hit.

- Some things like fall damage will ignore this and deal slight health damage.

### Strange Merchant

Magic Coffin [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - A jack of all trades, all attribute requirements are ignored. Activate your Coffin with C.

- Activating your Coffin takes 50. This Note cost will increase by 50 per Power, costing 1,000 at Power 20.
- When used, the Coffin acts like a loot box, randomly rolling an item to give you.
- There is a 1% chance to grant the Skull, giving you high tier loot. This chance is increased by 1% per level, up to 20% at power 20.
- There is a 5-10% chance to roll a blank, doubling the chest of the next box. This can stack.
- After defeating [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King), you gain the Know When to Run Talent. This adds the entire [Depths](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/The_Depths_(Vow_of_Iron)) lootpool is added to the Coffin, but the Coffin's price is increased by 50%.
- Coffin activation uses your Resonance keybind.
- You can use any weapon or equipment item without meeting their attribute requirements.
- Prerequisite: [Strange Merchant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Strange_Merchant) Memento

### The Emperor's Blade

True Mirage Clone [Rare Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Successfully dodging leaves behind a heat mirage clone that sets enemies that swung at you on fire.

- Has a 20s Cooldown
- Prerequisite: [Flame Worshipper](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Flame_Worshipper) Memento, defeat [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity)

### Theros Disciple

Spark of Theros [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your Thundercall is imbued with the spark of Theros, altering its appearance and properties.

- Turns your Thundercall white.
- Changes the [Shock](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shock) status effect to [Blightshock](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Blightshock), applying [Wither](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Wither).
- Prerequisites: [Theros Disciple](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Theros_Disciple) Memento, defeat [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King)

Storm Return [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Being under lightning/wind status effect causes your Basic Attacks and Criticals to do 10% more damage. (Amplified with [Lightning Cloak](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Lightning_Cloak))

- Being under the effects of Amped or Lightning Cloak will proc this effect.
- Prerequisites: [Theros Disciple](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Theros_Disciple) Memento, purchased from the starting shop

### Toxic Personality

Compelling Offer [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Any charmed enemy below 50% hit with your mantras or critical will mysteriously begin attacking everyone but you. [[Madness](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Madness)] Your [taunt](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Taunt) also now functions as a counter that stacks damage multipliers with every successful taunt.

- Madness from this Talent procs when attacking charmed enemies who are below 50% health.
- Madness prevents non-boss enemies from dealing damage and hitstunning you.
- Using Taunt as a counter displays [Prediction](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Prediction)'s visual effects but with a dark purple color. This also plays a laughing sound effect and reduces Taunt's cooldown to 1 second.
- Taunt counter does not deal damage, but instead stacks Taunt's damage buff effect.
- Prerequisites: [Strange Merchant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Strange_Merchant) Memento, purchased from the starting shop

Jackpot [Common Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Charmed enemies now have a chance to be hit critically, multiplying the damage.

- There is a ?% chance that your hits against a Charmed target will be a critical hit, dealing significantly more damage.
- Prerequisites: [Strange Merchant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Strange_Merchant) Memento, purchased from the Power 20 shop

Sweeten the Deal [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - [Madness](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Madness) can now be applied to enemies at 80% health or lower.

- Prerequisites: [Strange Merchant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Strange_Merchant) Memento, purchased from the Power 10 shop

- Taunting Tongue [Common Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your [taunt](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Taunt) now applies [Madness](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Madness).

Voice of the Mad God [Common Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Any time you apply [charm](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Charm) it now applies " [Madness](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Madness)" to enemies directly.

- Prerequisites: [Strange Merchant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Strange_Merchant) Memento, defeat [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity)

### Unique

Precision Hitter [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Your Basic Attack posture damage is increased by 15%.

- Prerequisites: [Saint Jay](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Saint_Jay) Memento, purchased from the Power 5 shop

### Void Glutton

Void Glutton [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Siphon some of your enemies ether when you kill them. This gets added onto your health permanently, however you are now slowed at all times. This slow increases the more health you have siphoned.

- Gives 2 maximum health when you kill a player. You will also be slowed, with the slow being based on how much maximum health you've siphoned.
- On proc a message at the top of your screen will display: "You siphon some of your victim's ether, netting you x more health."
- Prerequisite: [Void Glutton](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Void_Glutton) Memento

Dark Harvest [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Steal all of the hunger of enemies caught in your [Shadow Vortex](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shadow_Vortex). Knocking a player also greatly enhances your next Shadow Vortex.

- Prerequisites: [Void Glutton](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Void_Glutton) Memento, defeat [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King)

Erupting Shade [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - The size of your [shadow eruption](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shadow_Eruption) increases with how much siphoned health you have gained from wiping players.

- Prerequisites: [Void Glutton](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Void_Glutton) Memento, defeat [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity)

Ether Repletion [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Hunger stolen from other players now grants you ether as well.

- Hunger stolen from players through any means will proc this Talent.
- Prerequisites: [Void Glutton](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Void_Glutton) Memento, defeat [True Heart of Enmity](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/True_Heart_of_Enmity)

Hearty Ether [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - [Devouring Eye](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Devouring_Eye) steal procs now heal you based on the amount of ether stolen.

- Prerequisites: [Void Glutton](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Void_Glutton) Memento, defeat [Dravik, The Rat King](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Dravik,_The_Rat_King)

Null Recovery [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - Sacrifice 20% of your hunger bar to recover 10% of your health back.

- Grants a Talent tool.
- Prerequisites: [Void Glutton](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Void_Glutton) Memento, defeat [Shogun of the Prophet's Guard (Temple of Mur)](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Shogun_of_the_Prophet%27s_Guard_(Temple_of_Mur))

### Miscellaneous

Pleeksty's Inheritance [Memento Talent, [Vow of Iron](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Vow_of_Iron) Exclusive] - You inherit the teachings of Amorus Pleeksty, augmenting your flamecharm mantras.

- Makes your Flamecharm Mantras extremely loud.
- Prerequisite: [Flame Worshipper](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Flame_Worshipper) Memento

### Weapon Talents

Not to be confused with Talents that have a weapon requirement, these Talents will automatically be given to you upon equipping specific [weapons](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Weapons), but only exist while these weapons are equipped; using any ability that changes your equipped weapon will temporarily remove these Talents from your character. These Talents are often very potent, as they are the unique traits of the weapons themselves, though not every weapon with a unique trait has a Talent card to display it. Weapon Talents are listed in alphabetical order:

- Blade Threading: [Ferractine](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Ferractine)- Landing Critical attacks applies metal rod and slow.

- Hex Bullet: [Duskshot](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Duskshot)- Bullets that hit your opponents block shatter into dust on impact, unleashing a maddening smoke that increases your opponent's sanity.

- Monster Slayer: [Darksteel Cleaver](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Darksteel_Cleaver)- Deal 25% more damage to giant enemies.

- Night Night: [Night Star](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Night_Star)- Uppercut attacks blind opponents for 15 seconds.

Reforged Alloy: Unknown - Anytime you consume iron rods using Metal Pull or any Rending Needle technique, reduce the cooldown of your next critical based on the amount of rods consumed.

- This Talent does not work.
- Despite being in the Weapon category, this Talent is given as a starting Talent of the [Iron Vessel](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Iron_Vessel) Memento.

- Reverse Necrosis: [Saint Jay's Hammer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Saint_Jay%27s_Hammer)- Landing any mantra will now heal you based on how slow your opponent is.

- Sadistic Split: [Depthsplitter](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Depthsplitter)- Guardbreaking your opponent now heals you. The amount of this heal is based on how much insanity your character currently has.

Shatter: [Icepick](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Icepick)- Landing a critical shatters your opponent, ridding them of their speed debuffs for 5 frost damage per debuff.

- Landing a critical attack removes all speed debuffs your opponent has, dealing 5 Frostdraw damage per debuff.

### Equipment Talents

Certain Talents are given when a specific equipment or outfit is worn. Multiple copies of the same Talent do not stack. Equipment Talents are listed in alphabetical order:

Analyze: [Armored Sensor Mask](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Armored_Sensor_Mask)- Marking enemies with your Callout analyzes them, making them take 20% more damage from all sources for 10 seconds.

- 45 second cooldown.

- Beginner's Luck: [Beginner's Brace](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Beginner%27s_Brace)- Slightly increase the amount of loot you find in chests.

Blend In: [Enforcer Helm](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Enforcer_Helm)- You resemble one of the Enforcers, become unnoticeable by regular depths mobs.

- Most monsters in [The Depths](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/The_Depths_(Vow_of_Iron)) will be neutral you while you have this equipment on.

- Clutch: [Reinforced Authority Plate](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Reinforced_Authority_Plate)- For every combat tag you acquire, gain 4% more posture resistance.

- Conditioning: [Veteran Ranger's Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Veteran_Ranger%27s_Boots)- Reduce all slows you receive by 40%.

Cursed Immortality: [Corrupted Immortal Helm](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Corrupted_Immortal_Helm)- Instead of getting knocked, get set to 75% health. When this effect occurrs, this guarantees that you depths trial will become corrupted for the rest of your character's run.

- Instead of being knocked, your health will be set to 75% of its maximum instead, with a 5 minute cooldown.
- Once this Talent procs, all future [Depths Trials](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Depths_Trial) will automatically be [Corrupted](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Corrupted_Monsters) for the rest of your character's life.
- This Talent does not proc during the [Zi'eer](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Zi%27eer) boss fight.

Emergency Rations: [Veteran Ranger's Brace](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Veteran_Ranger%27s_Brace)- Eat some of your emergency rations, healing you for 20 health. You can only use this 3 times before having to sit at a campfire outside of combat again.

- Grants a Talent tool with 3 charges.
- Using the Talent tool consumes one of the charges to heal you for 20 flat health. This has a 15 second cooldown.
- Resting at a campfire, while not in combat, restores all charges.

Ether Efficiency: [Caster Fur Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Caster_Fur_Boots)- All mantras you cast now have a reduced cost of 30 ether.

- Applies a flat -30 Ether cost to any Mantra you cast.

- Ether Upgrade: [Caster Fur Pauldrons](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Caster_Fur_Pauldrons)- All mantras you cast now get one bonus level on top of their current level.

Experienced Healer: [Cultist Cowl](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Cultist_Cowl)- Everytime you heal yourself, gain 5 extra health on top of that.

- 10 second cooldown.

Fictitious Force: [Corrupted Duelist's Mask](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Corrupted_Duelist%27s_Mask)- Reduce the power of all speed boosts you gain by 50%. Deal extra true damage based on how fast you are.

- Halves the effectiveness of all speed boosts.
- Adds true damage to your attacks, with the true damage scaling on your speed boosts and momentum.

Flaredrop: [Flare Drop Earrings](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Flare_Drop_Earrings)- Landing mantras sets your opponent on fire.

- 5 second cooldown.

Flask Amplifier: [Potion Master's Hat](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Potion_Master%27s_Hat)- Heal an extra 15% from flasks.

- Increases the healing gained from [Etris Flasks](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Etris_Flask) by 15% additively, meaning Flasks will restore 65% health on use instead of 50%.

- Gale Boots: [Gale Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Gale_Boots)- Reduce the amount of fall damage you take by 50%.

Gunslinger's Fury: [Bounty Hunter's Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Bounty_Hunter%27s_Boots)- When having a side gun equipped with a gun or rifle, use a critical in the air to shoot a barrage of bullets towards your opponent.

- If your main weapon is a Pistol or a Rifle, and you have an offhand Pistol equipped, your critical will be replaced with the [Soulwrought Gun](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Soulwrought_Gun)'s special attack if used midair.

Haste: [Trackstar Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Trackstar_Boots)- Gain a permanent +3 speed buff at all times.

- Increases your movement speed by +3 studs per second.
- This does not count as a speed boost for Talent procs.

- Hellion Shift: [Oni Mask](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Oni_Mask)- The more insane you are the more iframes you gain on your dodge.

Heretic's Sacrifice: [Heretic's Moonseye Gauntlets](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Heretic%27s_Moonseye_Gauntlets)- A chant that heavily sacrifices your sanity to regain a bit of health.

- Grants a Talent tool that drains a significant amount of sanity to heal 20 raw health.
- If you are already at 0 Sanity, you will still be healed at no downside.

Iron Stance: [Depths Wanderer's Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Depths_Wanderer%27s_Boots)- Be able to shrug off being ragdolled every so often.

- Grants one instance of ragdoll immunity with a 20 second cooldown.

- Merc Blade: [Mercenary's Hood](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Mercenary%27s_Hood)- For every 10% stealth you have, deal 1 extra damage on all sources of damage.

- Navae's Connection: [Blessed Nomad Pendant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Blessed_Nomad_Pendant)- All [Navaen related combat weapons](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Navae_Weapons) are empowered.

Natural Healing: [Lifeweaver's Hat](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Lifeweaver%27s_Hat)- Every time you eat plants, heal 2% of your health.

- Heal 2% of your maximum health whenever you eat a plant-based [Food](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Food) item.

- Omen: [Corrupted Pathfinder Elite](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Corrupted_Pathfinder_Elite)- Your Shadow Travel now has no windup and always requires health to teleport.

Pace: [Trackstar Pauldrons](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Trackstar_Pauldrons)- Hitting opponents apply a speed boost. The faster you are the less damage you take.

- Grants damage reduction based on the collective potency on all speed boosts you have.
- Landing attacks grants a speed boost.

Piercing Shot: [Bounty Hunter's Garb](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Bounty_Hunter%27s_Garb)- Activate to make your next critical attack with any gun have 100% chip and shatter your opponent's armor for a few seconds, making them take increased PEN from all sources.

- Grants a Talent tool, that on use, enhances your next critical attack used with any [Rifle](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Rifles) or [Pistol](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Pistols).
- Enhanced critical attacks gain 100% chip damage and apply the [Pierced Armor](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Status_Effects#Pierced_Armor) status effect to your opponent, increasing incoming PEN for a few seconds.

- Poisoned Knife: [Rogue Assassin's Hood](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Rogue_Assassin%27s_Hood)- Your Deepwound's anti heal now lasts 60 seconds on assassination instead of 20.

Pyreborne: [Emberseal Pendant](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Emberseal_Pendant)- Your Flamecharm mantras have all stats amplified

- Increases the base damage of all Flamecharm Mantras by 30%

Risky Defense: [Alloyed Phalanx Plate](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Alloyed_Phalanx_Plate)- Decrease how much posture damage you take from physical attacks by 25%, but take 50% more posture damage from mantras.

- Increases incoming posture damage from Mantras by 50%.
- Decreases incoming posture damage from weapon attacks by 25%.

Royal Charge: [Royal Commander Helm](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Royal_Commander_Helm)- When landing a running attack while using a club to gain a bit of temp health.

- Running attacks with [Club](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Clubs) weapons grant [Temporary Health](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Temporary_Health).
- 20 second cooldown.

- Safety First: [Beginner's Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Beginner%27s_Boots)- At the start of fights, gain 25% damage resistance for 30 seconds.

- Sky Aid: [Sky Warrior Helm](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Sky_Warrior_Helm)- Take 80% less damage while in the air.

- Slick: [Konga's Parka](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Konga%27s_Parka)- Coat of the infamous thief Emiya Konga. Improves the user's sliding abilities. This effect stacks with [Konga's Clutch Ring](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Konga%27s_Clutch_Ring).

Steel Grip: [Reforged Gauntlets](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Reforged_Gauntlets)- When you are blockbroken, negate it and set your posture to 80%.

- 90 second cooldown.

Tempo Sap: [Prophet's Operative Cloak](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Prophet%27s_Operative_Cloak)- Activate to make your dagger's light attacks steal tempo per hit for 20 seconds. [Dagger]

- 1 minute cooldown.

- The Rich Get Richer: [Prosperous Gumshoe Longcoat](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Prosperous_Gumshoe_Longcoat)- Gain 15% more notes whenever you sell something.

Vessel's Gamble: [Corrupted Vessel's Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Corrupted_Vessel%27s_Boots)- When you get guardbroken you take 3 times the damage of the attack.

- Though this Talent is actively detrimental to the user, the Corrupted Vessel's Boots +20 Posture offsets the downside.

- Weather Resistant: [Extra Thick Overcoat](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Extra_Thick_Overcoat)- Take 30% less damage when under less than ideal weather conditions.

### Set Talents

Certain Talents are given when you equip a full equipment set, though not all equipment sets have unique Set Talents. These Talents are exclusive to that equipment set and are unobtainable by any other means. Set Talents are listed in alphabetical order:

Explosive Rounds: [Bounty Hunter Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)- Every bullet that lands now detonates on your opponent.

- Bounty Hunter Set: [Bounty Hunter's Garb](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Bounty_Hunter%27s_Garb), [Bounty Hunter's Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Bounty_Hunter%27s_Boots)

Hunting Trap: [Veteran Ranger Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)- Place down a trap that upon being stepped on will render your opponent unable to move for a bit.

- Grants a Talent tool that places a bear trap on use. 60 second cooldown.
- Walking onto a bear trap deals high damage and renders you immobile for a few seconds.
- The bear trap cannot trigger again once it has been triggered.
- You can trigger your own bear trap.
- Veteran Ranger Set: [Veteran Ranger's Brace](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Veteran_Ranger%27s_Brace), [Veteran Ranger's Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Veteran_Ranger%27s_Boots)

Overdrive: [Caster Fur Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)- Go beyond your limits. All mantras you cast deal twice as much damage for 10 seconds.

- Grants a Talent tool that doubles the damage of your Mantras for 10 seconds. 3 minute cooldown.
- Caster Fur Set: [Caster Fur Pauldrons](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Caster_Fur_Pauldrons), [Caster Fur Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Caster_Fur_Boots)

Rush: [Trackstar Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)- Massively reduce your dodge cooldown.

- Trackstar Set: [Trackstar Pauldrons](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Trackstar_Pauldrons), [Trackstar Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Trackstar_Boots)

Training Wheels: [Beginner's Set](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Equipment_with_Set_Bonuses)- Increase the amount of parry frames you have by 20%.

- Beginner's Set: [Beginner's Brace](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Beginner%27s_Brace), [Beginner's Boots](https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Beginner%27s_Boots)

## Trivia

The Talent, Ultrakill, breaks the fourth wall by requesting the player to play ULTRAKILL.

- Ironically, it then proceeds to write the name of the game incorrectly, as ULTRAKILL is meant to be written in all capitals.
